# CHANGELOG

## Hotfix pre-deploy (plan 2026-09-28): re-accept Termeni amanat pentru conturile existente — 2026-10-04
- **Obiectiv** (decizie PO 2026-10-04): re-acceptarea fortata a Termenilor/Politicii (E-admin-platform-2) e amanata. Doar conturile noi accepta textele 2026-09-29, la inregistrare. Conturile existente vor fi intrebate abia dupa revizuirea avocatului. Comutarea se face din setari, fara release nou.
- **Comportament**:
  - Cheie noua `system_settings.legal_reaccept_existing_users`, valori `'true'`/`'false'`, implicit `'false'`.
  - Cu `'false'`, `needsLegalAcceptance` e `false` pentru toti: conturile cu versiuni NULL (existente) si cele cu o versiune veche. Dialogul blocant nu mai apare.
  - Cu `'true'` revine comportamentul E-admin-platform-2: versiune NULL sau diferita de cea curenta → dialog. Adminii raman exceptati in ambele cazuri.
  - Register salveaza in continuare versiunile curente. `POST /legal/accept` ramane neschimbat (acceptarea voluntara inregistreaza versiunile).
  - Valoarea se citeste prin `SettingsService` si se tine in memorie 60 s in `AuthService`. Userii la zi si adminii nu citesc setarea deloc, deci `/auth/me` nu adauga un query per cerere. Comutarea ajunge pe ambele sloturi in maxim 60 s.
  - Login si `/auth/me` folosesc aceeasi regula. Refresh nu intoarce flagul.
- **Fisiere**:
  - DB: migrare de date `apps/backend/prisma/migrations/20261004120000_legal_reaccept_setting/migration.sql` (`INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, rollback descris in SQL, fara schimbare de schema) si `apps/backend/prisma/seed.ts`.
  - Backend: `src/modules/auth/auth.service.ts`, `src/modules/legal/legal.constants.ts`, `src/common/settings/setting-defaults.ts`, `src/modules/admin/admin.service.ts` (accepta doar `'true'`/`'false'`), `src/modules/audit/audit-snapshot.ts` (comutarea apare in audit).
  - Teste: `src/modules/auth/auth.service.spec.ts`, `src/modules/admin/admin-settings-validation.spec.ts`, `src/modules/audit/audit-snapshot.spec.ts`.
  - Altele: comentariu in `apps/frontend/src/app/[locale]/_components/legal-acceptance-gate.tsx` (fara schimbare de UI) si `docs/04-data-model.md`.
- **Cum se porneste** (dupa revizuirea avocatului): Admin → Setari → sectiunea „Setari de sistem”, cheia `legal_reaccept_existing_users` = `true`, Salveaza. In maxim 60 s, conturile cu versiuni NULL/vechi primesc dialogul la urmatorul `/auth/me`. Oprirea: aceeasi cheie = `false`.
- **Teste**:
  - `pnpm -F backend test -- auth admin-settings-validation audit-snapshot`: 6 suite, 195 teste trecute (o suita de integrare sarita fara `TEST_DATABASE_URL`).
  - Cazuri noi: helperul pe ambele valori, `me()`/login pe cont NULL (implicit → false, `'true'` → true), versiune veche, ADMIN, cache de 60 s si `GET /auth/me` controller + service real → `needsLegalAcceptance=false` pe cont NULL cu setarea implicita.
  - `pnpm -F shared build && pnpm typecheck`: 0. `pnpm lint`: 0.
  - Suita completa backend (`pnpm -F backend test`): 103 suite / 1297 teste trecute, exit 0 (13 suite de integrare sarite fara `TEST_DATABASE_URL`).
  - Migrarea e aplicata local doar pe ea (`prisma db execute` + `migrate resolve --applied`), ca sa nu aplice si `20261002160000_furniture_catalog_seven`, care asteapta aprobare explicita. Randul exista in DB-ul local cu valoarea `false`.
- **Checklist de acceptare**:
  - [x] Cheia apare in Admin → Setari (lista generica `system_settings`) si accepta doar `true`/`false`.
  - [x] Cont existent (versiuni NULL) + setarea implicita → `/auth/me` si login intorc `needsLegalAcceptance=false`.
  - [x] Cu `'true'` → comportamentul de azi. ADMIN exceptat in ambele cazuri.
  - [x] Register si `POST /legal/accept` inregistreaza in continuare versiunile curente.
  - [ ] Pe prod: migrarea se aplica la boot, la deploy. PO comuta cheia pe `true` dupa revizuirea avocatului.

## Seven configurable furniture types — 2026-10-02
- Added sideboard, wall cabinet, bathroom vanity, entryway unit, sofa, armchair and ottoman as distinct Studio and request types. Primary-source research informs their dimensions, storage, mounting, seating and upholstery controls; RO/EN labels, previews and summaries preserve those choices.
- Added lightweight parametric seating, vessel basins, plumbing-clearance drawer trays, entryway fittings and opening ottoman lids. Material batching, procedural fabrics and overview geometry preserve on-demand rendering; moving storage stays framed without resetting the camera.
- Wall cabinet elevation participates in room/house rendering, furniture overlap, ceiling checks and publication preflight after questionnaire edits. Existing draft recovery remains readable.
- Default seating uses 3 main draw calls; all seven default fixtures record zero idle draws. Sofa/chair/ottoman buffer allocations stay stable across repeated resizing and release on editor close. Largest tested chaise/double-vanity examples remain below 9,000 main-view triangles.
- Validation and research: `docs/implementation/furniture-catalog-2026-10-02.md`. Additive enum migration prepared; sizing initialization awaits the user's business choice. No migration applied, commit or deployment.

## Lightweight realism across all furniture — 2026-10-02
- Extended the bed refinement approach to all ten furniture types: compact rounded board edges, non-repeating metric wood grain and consistent drawer interiors; tables and benches gain shaped tops and tapered square legs, and upholstered benches gain a supported cushion with attached piping.
- Batched cabinet drawer/tilt assemblies without changing their motion. Overview rendering drops unused procedural attributes and material splits; shared board geometry also reduces the cost of room opening details.
- All ten default editor fixtures use 24–69% fewer triangles. Wardrobe draw calls fall 91→43, dresser 83→35 and desk 43→19. Saved configurations, quoted dimensions, sliders and options are unchanged. No new image textures or idle render loop.
- Acceptance and before/after measurements: `docs/implementation/furniture-realism-2026-10-02.md`. Implemented locally; no commit or deployment.

## Hanging-rod hangers — 2026-10-02
- Added one slim wooden clothes hanger with a metal hook per actual hanging rod, including stacked rods. Its shoulder span runs across cabinet depth and clamps to the available space behind the fronts; its position follows its rod when dimensions/options change.
- Shared derived details feed the piece editor and room/house/viewer renderer. Hangers are visual context only: no saved configuration, BOM, pricing or database changes. Meshes are batched, with fewer than 500 triangles per detailed hanger and no new textures or animation.
- Acceptance: [x] 492 shared tests; [x] 120 focused frontend 3D tests; [x] frontend typecheck/lint; [x] native wardrobe capture and current live configuration inspected with no rendering errors. Local only, uncommitted.

## Lightweight bed realism — 2026-10-02
- Replaced stacked bedding slabs with a draped duvet and matching throw, softer pillows with integrated seams and convex upholstered headboard pads. Shapes regenerate from existing mattress/options data and remain inside the current footprint, clear of drawer travel.
- Batched each moving drawer by material. In the measured detail views, default/upholstered triangles changed 8,450 → 8,114 / 15,978 → 13,382; drawer-bed main draw calls changed 35 → 23. No texture/material or idle-render increase. Drawer buffers trade an additional 0.52 MiB for fewer submissions.
- Shared 473 tests, focused frontend 115 tests, frontend typecheck/lint and desktop/mobile control checks pass; bed motion/camera checks pass. Acceptance and limits: `docs/implementation/bed-realism-2026-10-02.md`.
- Local only; no saved-schema, DB, pricing, commit or deployment changes.

## Parametric 3D furniture refinement — 2026-10-02
- Improved all ten furniture families with richer wood/fabric materials, mounted brass handles, shelf supports and rod sockets. Hinged fronts carry size-aware hinge details; sliding fronts use recessed pulls. Hardware derives from the current panels and follows existing motion.
- Beds gain thinner bedding and attached seam piping; round tables gain a shaped pedestal; extendable tops show their joining seam; upholstered/storage/shoe benches gain tailored cushions, a visible grip or slatted shelving.
- Piece editor, room/house scenes, request viewer and snapshots share consistent finishes and hardware. Room openings gain refined frames and fittings. Detailed and overview rendering retain separate geometry/material budgets.
- Existing sliders, finish choices and storage/front options remain authoritative. No saved-configuration schema, database, pricing or backend changes.
- Validation: shared 473 tests; frontend 419 tests; frontend typecheck/lint; six desktop/mobile Playwright checks; four furniture motion cases; 20 native WebGL gallery captures; repeated edit/reopen GPU resource check. Runtime caveat and acceptance details: `docs/implementation/parametric-3d-refinement-2026-10-02.md`.
- Local changes only; no commit or deployment.

## Sprint 76 (plan 2026-09-28): Collections into requests (P3) — 2026-10-01
- **Obiectiv**: inchiderea sprintului pachetului E-client-34 (finding R2-INSPO-ADMIN-11). Clientul ataseaza la cerere o colectie intreaga (pana la 10 poze), cu notita (max 200) si camera per poza. Firma vede inspiratia grupata pe camere: notita e mascata in marketplace si bruta pe fisa de lucru doar cu acces la contacte. Aceeasi poza se poate salva in mai multe colectii. Detaliile pachetului sunt in intrarea de mai jos.
- **Impact**:
  - DB: migrare aditiva `20261001070000_inspiration_notes_room_links`: 4 coloane nullable, 1 index si 1 FK `ON DELETE SET NULL`. Rollback-ul e descris in SQL.
  - Backend: publish/edit accepta `inspirationPhotos`, iar `inspirationPhotoIds` ramane acceptat. Notitele trec prin screening (`PHOTO_NOTE`). Endpoint nou: `PATCH /inspiration/boards/:id/items/:photoId`. `inspirationPhotos` apare in marketplace (mascat), pe fisa de lucru (brut sau mascat) si e gol in REDACTED/TOMBSTONE.
  - Frontend: pickerul are „Adaugă toată colecția” si carduri cu notita si camera. `RequestInspirationByRoom` e folosit pe cele 3 pagini. SaveButton lucreaza cu mai multe colectii, iar colectia are editor de notita. Fix de la verificare: un rand din `BoardPicker` e dezactivat cat timp mutatia lui ruleaza, iar `toggleBoard` ignora clicul dublu, ca sa nu plece POST si DELETE in paralel pe aceeasi pereche.
  - i18n: +31 chei RO+EN, −2 chei moarte. Paritate 3617/3617.
  - Preturile si regulile de business nu se schimba. docs/07 ramane neatins.
- **Fisiere** (inchidere): `apps/frontend/src/app/[locale]/inspiration/_components/{board-picker,save-button}.tsx` (fixul verificatorului) si `CHANGELOG.md`. Lista completa a pachetului e in intrarea de mai jos.
- **Securitate (security-close peste diff-ul sprintului, re-rulat la inchidere)**:
  - Authz pe endpointuri noi: `PATCH /inspiration/boards/:id/items/:photoId` trece prin JwtAuthGuard global (fara `@Public`), `ParseUUIDPipe` pe ambii parametri si `requireOwned` (colectia altui user → 404). `roomLocalId` e validat doar contra camerelor procesate ale aceleiasi cereri (PASS).
  - Validare input: `BoardItemMetaDto` (`MaxLength 200`, `IsEnum(RoomType)`, patch gol → 400) si `RequestInspirationPhotoInputDto` (`IsUUID`, `MaxLength 200`, `roomLocalId` 1..60), cu `ArrayMaxSize 10` + `ValidateNested`. Se adauga zod in shared si `VARCHAR(200)` in DB. `forbidNonWhitelisted` e activ global (PASS).
  - Idempotenta / @Audit: nu exista mutatii noi pe bani, claim sau accept. Colectiile nu erau auditate nici inainte (N/A).
  - PII in loguri: nu exista loguri noi cu notite. `request_contact_detections` pastreaza doar un fragment partial ascuns. In fisierele noi FE/BE nu exista `console.*`, `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` sau `localStorage`/`sessionStorage` nou; notitele stau doar in draftul local deja existent al configuratorului, ca descrierea (PASS).
  - Invarianta 4.2: marketplace-ul mascheaza mereu notita. Fisa de lucru o arata bruta doar cu `clientVisible`, iar in REDACTED/TOMBSTONE intoarce `[]`. `configuratorState` si snapshoturile de versiune nu ajung la firme (PASS).
  - Secrete: nimic nou in cod, seed sau `.env.example` (PASS).
  - Soft-delete: `detail`, `list` si `_count` filtreaza pozele nepublicate sau sterse si firmele sterse (PASS). Lista de poze a cererii ramane nefiltrata la citire, ca `inspirationPhotoIds` inainte; pozele sunt filtrate de galerie la afisare (neschimbat).
  - Cookies/CORS: neschimbate (PASS).
  - Paritate i18n: flatten node ro.json vs en.json da 3617/3617, 0 chei lipsa pe oricare parte, 0 aparitii „atelier” (PASS).
  - Nu a fost nevoie de niciun patch de securitate la inchidere.
- **Masuratori la inchidere**: `pnpm -F shared build && pnpm typecheck` a iesit cu 0 (shared, backend, frontend). `pnpm lint` a iesit cu 0. La verificare: jest backend 103 suite / 1272 teste (13 suite de integrare sarite fara `TEST_DATABASE_URL`). Cele 2 spec-uri de integrare requests au trecut pe Postgres local (6 teste). Vitest FE: 63 fisiere / 408 teste. Shared: 14 fisiere / 401 teste.

    - Aceeasi poza in doua colectii → `GET /inspiration/boards/saved` are ambele perechi.
    - `PATCH .../items/:photoId` → 200 cu notita taiata de spatii. `{}` → 400. 201 caractere → 400. Colectia altui user → 404. `move` pastreaza notita.
    - Publish cu `inspirationPhotos` → `request_room_id` = id-ul camerei persistate. Cu ambele campuri → 400. Cu `roomLocalId` necunoscut → 400.
    - `GET /marketplace/requests/:id` → notita mascata. `GET /quotes/by-claim/:slot` → notita bruta cu acces.

De verificat in RO si EN, la 1280 px si 375 px.

## Sprint 76 (plan 2026-09-28): Collections into requests (P3) — 2026-10-01 (raport de implementare)
- **Pachet**: E-client-34 (finding R2-INSPO-ADMIN-11). Clientul ataseaza o colectie intreaga la cerere, cu notita si camera per poza; firma vede inspiratia grupata pe camere; o poza se salveaza in mai multe colectii.
- **Obiectiv**: DW1 pickerul ofera „Adaugă toată colecția” (pana la 10 poze) si pastreaza notitele colectiei; DW2 vederea firmei (marketplace pre-claim + fisa de lucru) arata notitele pozelor grupate pe camere; DW3 aceeasi poza in doua colectii din acelasi popover.
- **Impact**:
  - DB: migrare aditiva `20261001070000_inspiration_notes_room_links` (4 coloane nullable: `inspiration_board_items.note/room_type`, `request_inspiration_photos.note/request_room_id`, 1 index, 1 FK `ON DELETE SET NULL`); fara backfill; rollback documentat in SQL.
  - Shared: `INSPIRATION_NOTE_MAX` (200), `MAX_REQUEST_INSPIRATION_PHOTOS` (10), `boardItemMetaSchema`, `requestInspirationPhotoInputSchema`, `RequestInspirationPhotoDto`, `inspirationPhotos` pe `RequestDto` / `MarketplaceDetailDto` / `ClaimRequestDetailDto`, `InspirationBoardDetailDto.items`, `CONTACT_DETECTION_FIELDS` + `PHOTO_NOTE`.
  - Backend: publish/edit primesc `inspirationPhotos: [{ photoId, note?, roomLocalId? }]` (`inspirationPhotoIds` ramane acceptat; ambele → 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR`); helper pur `requests/inspiration-selection.ts`; screening `PHOTO_NOTE` (MASK → rand `MASKED` / BLOCK → 400 `REQUEST_CONTACT_INFO_IN_TEXT` cu detaliu `{ field: 'inspirationNote', photoId }`); camera legata prin harta `sourceLocalId → id` din `writeRooms` (numar constant de query-uri); marketplace mascheaza mereu notita, fisa de lucru o arata bruta doar cu acces la contacte, REDACTED/TOMBSTONE → `[]`; boards: `detail.items`, `PATCH /inspiration/boards/:id/items/:photoId` (requireOwned), `moveItem` muta si notita.
  - Frontend: store cu `inspirationMeta` (+ `addInspirationPhotos` / `setInspirationNote` / `setInspirationRoom`), picker cu „Adaugă toată colecția” + carduri notita/camera (`InspirationSelectionCard`), review trimite `inspirationPhotos`, hidratare la editare, `RequestInspirationByRoom` inlocuieste `RequestInspirationStrip` (sters) pe cele 3 pagini, SaveButton multi-colectie cu bife + rollback per pereche in hook-uri, editor de notita pe detaliul colectiei (`BoardItemNoteEditor`).
  - i18n: +31 chei RO+EN (Configurator.inspirationPicker, Configurator.validation.inspirationRoomUnknown, Inspiration, Admin.contactDetections.fields.PHOTO_NOTE), −2 chei moarte (`Inspiration.saved`, `Inspiration.removeFromBoard`); paritate 3617/3617 (3588 + 31 - 2).
  - Blue/green: imaginea veche ignora coloanele; o editare prin imaginea veche (rollback) rescrie pozele fara notite/camere (pierdere acceptata); un bundle FE nou pe API vechi primeste 400 pe `inspirationPhotos` (fereastra scurta, imagine combinata FE+BE).
  - Fara schimbari de preturi/reguli de business; docs/07 neatins.
- **Fisiere**: vezi raportul de implementare; principalele — `apps/backend/prisma/schema.prisma`, migrarea de mai sus, `apps/backend/src/modules/requests/{inspiration-selection,contact-screening,requests.service}.ts`, `modules/inspiration/{boards.service,inspiration.controller}.ts`, `modules/marketplace/marketplace.service.ts`, `modules/quotes/quotes.service.ts`, `packages/shared/src/{inspiration,request,marketplace,quote}.schemas.ts`, `contact-detection.ts`, `apps/frontend/src/stores/configurator-store.ts`, `components/configurator/{inspiration-picker,inspiration-selection-card,request-inspiration-by-room,review-step,configurator-wizard}.tsx`, `app/[locale]/inspiration/**`, `hooks/use-inspiration-boards.ts`, `messages/{ro,en}.json`, `docs/04-data-model.md`.
- **Securitate (security-close peste diff)**:
  - Authz: `PATCH /inspiration/boards/:id/items/:photoId` trece prin `requireOwned` (404 pe colectia altui user); `roomLocalId` se valideaza contra camerelor procesate ale aceleiasi cereri (nu se poate lega o camera straina) (PASS).
  - Invarianta 4.2: notitele sunt text liber → screening la publish/edit (`PHOTO_NOTE`), mascare mereu in marketplace pre-claim, text brut pe fisa de lucru doar cu `clientVisible`, `inspirationPhotos: []` in REDACTED/TOMBSTONE; log-ul de detectii pastreaza doar fragment partial ascuns (PASS).
  - Validare: class-validator (`MaxLength 200`, `ArrayMaxSize 10`, nested) + zod + `VARCHAR(200)`; `forbidNonWhitelisted` global (PASS).
  - Idempotenta / @Audit: fara mutatii noi de bani/claim/accept; colectiile nu sunt auditate azi (N/A).
  - PII in loguri: fara loguri noi cu notite; fara `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`/`localStorage` nou in afara draftului existent (PASS).
  - Soft-delete: `detail` pe colectie pastreaza filtrul pe poze publicate/nesterse (PASS).
- **Plan de test manual**: API (curl cu cookie, conturi demo doar pe localhost): doua colectii + aceeasi poza in ambele → `GET /inspiration/boards/saved` are ambele perechi; `PATCH .../items/:photoId` cu `{ note, roomType }` → `GET /boards/:id` are `items[0].note`; patch pe colectia altui user 404, `{}` 400, nota de 201 caractere 400; `move` pastreaza notita. ## Sprint 75 (plan 2026-09-28): Design primitives and tokens (P3) — 2026-10-01

## Sprint 74 (plan 2026-09-28): Perimeter and supply chain (P3) — 2026-09-29
- **Pachet**: E-admin-platform-56 — adrese interne ascunse + dependinte reparate (P3).
- **Obiectiv**: health-ul public nu mai divulga slotul/SHA-ul, backendul din containerul combinat asculta doar pe loopback, hosturile necanonice primesc noindex, CSP strict cu nonce, iar lantul de aprovizionare (dependinte, imagine, CI) trece fara HIGH/CRITICAL. Fara migrare, fara chei i18n, fara schimbari de preturi/reguli; docs/07 neatins. Stergerea domeniilor Railway ale sloturilor ramane DECIZIE NECESARA PO.
- **Impact**:
  - Backend: `HealthController` — corpul public al `/health`, `/health/live`, `/health/ready` e DOAR `{status}`; `checks`/`release`/`client`/`timestamp` doar cu `X-Health-Token` = `HEALTH_DETAILS_TOKEN` (sha256 + `timingSafeEqual`, header array respins); header redactat in pino. Config nou: `HEALTH_DETAILS_TOKEN` (optional, min 32, gol = nesetat), `LISTEN_HOST` (doar IP) → `app.listen(port, host)`. `start-combined.sh`: `LISTEN_HOST=127.0.0.1`. NestJS 11.2 / Express 5 / @nestjs/config 4 / @nestjs/bullmq 11 / @nestjs/cli 11 / @types/express 5; nestjs-pino cu `forRoutes: '{*path}'` (fara LegacyRouteConverter). nodemailer 10.0.13 (tipuri proprii, `@types/nodemailer` scos), `prisma` mutat in `dependencies`.
  - Frontend: `middleware.ts` construieste CSP-ul per cerere (`lib/csp.ts`: nonce, `'strict-dynamic'`, fara `'unsafe-inline'` pe scripturi, `report-uri /csp-report` + `Reporting-Endpoints`); matcher extins (404-ul radacina `/xyz` fara redirect de limba, redirectul `/`→`/en` pe cookie pastrat). `[locale]/layout.tsx` si `app/not-found.tsx` apeleaza `headers()` (randare dinamica). Ruta noua `/csp-report` (logica in `lib/csp-report.ts`: 16 KB, 60/min, mereu 204, linie `csp_violation` cu directiva, originea blocata si calea mascata). `next.config.mjs`: CSP static scos; productie: HSTS + COOP `same-origin` + `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow` pe orice host diferit de `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL`. `lib/monitoring.ts`: SHA-ul in bundle doar cu DSN. img/connect-src raman largi pana la build arg-ul `NEXT_PUBLIC_STORAGE_ORIGIN` (+ `NEXT_PUBLIC_CSP_IMG_ORIGINS`). vitest 4.1 (+ vite 6.4.3 explicit) in frontend si shared.

Limita de rata mutata in `lib/monitoring-rate-limit.ts`.
  - Imagine/infra: `Dockerfile.app` — stagiu `proddeps` (`pnpm install --frozen-lockfile --prod --filter backend...` + `prisma generate`), runtime-ul copiaza `node_modules` de acolo; auto-verificare fara `vitest|vite|jest|@nestjs+cli|typescript-eslint`; runtime cu `apt-get -y upgrade` si fara npm/npx/corepack globale (sursa tuturor CVE-urilor Node din Trivy); ARG-uri `NEXT_PUBLIC_STORAGE_ORIGIN` / `NEXT_PUBLIC_CSP_IMG_ORIGINS` (si in `apps/frontend/Dockerfile`). `deploy/gateway/Dockerfile`: Caddy fixat prin digest.
  - CI: `permissions: contents: read`, actiuni fixate prin SHA (checkout v4.4.0, pnpm/action-setup v4.4.0, setup-node v4.4.0, upload-artifact v4.6.2), audit complet la `high` pe langa cel `--prod`, pas Trivy 0.74.0 (digest) HIGH/CRITICAL `--ignore-unfixed` pe `cozy-app:ci`, `.trivyignore.yaml` (gol, regula `statement` + `expired_at` ≤ 90 zile), workflow nou `codeql.yml` (codeql-action v4.38.2 prin SHA). Overrides noi: `engine.io >=6.6.10`, `brace-expansion` 1.x/2.x, `js-yaml` 3.x/4.x, `glob@10`, `picomatch@4`, `tmp`.

## Sprint 73 (plan 2026-09-28): Pricing data model (P3) — 2026-09-29
- **Obiectiv**: inchiderea sprintului care face planurile de abonament date editabile din Admin (cod unic, nume RO/EN, planuri de 0 lei, functii per plan) cu un singur serviciu de entitlements si o matrice unica de permisiuni in firma, FARA a schimba vreun pret, numar de credite, durata, trial sau seed. Grila „runda 4” (Start 0 / Ofertare 299 / Prioritate 599, cost claim 3..25, facturare recurenta) NU e in docs/07 → ramane DECIZIE NECESARA; docs/07 nu a fost atins. Detaliile pachetului sunt in intrarea E-admin-platform-58 de mai jos.
- **Impact**:
  - Backend: modul nou `modules/entitlements` (`EntitlementsService.forCompany/has/toDto`, `activeSubscriptionWhere()` = singurul predicat „abonament activ”, `PlanFeatureGuard` + `@RequirePlanFeature`); `SubscriptionActiveGuard` prin entitlements (403 `SUBSCRIPTION_INACTIVE` neschimbat, 403 `PLAN_FEATURE_REQUIRED` pe plan fara `MARKETPLACE_ACCESS`); `GET /billing/entitlements`; `POST /admin/plans` + `PATCH /admin/plans/:id` extins (pret >= 0, nume, functii); `trial_plan` validat pe codul unui plan ACTIV; `purchaseSubscription` pe plan de 0 lei → 409 `PLAN_NOT_PURCHASABLE` inainte de comanda/Stripe; TVA si total calculate in backend (`billing/vat.ts`, Decimal half-up) pe `GET /billing/plans` si `/billing/credit-packages`; `CompanyManagerGuard`, `CompaniesService.requireManager`, `ClaimsService.assign` si `billingEmail` citesc matricea `can()` (aceleasi roluri ca inainte).
  - Frontend: portofel cu totaluri din backend (fara calcul TVA in FE), plan gratuit fara buton de cumparare, linia de acces la marketplace, linia de gating ascunsa pe planurile fara acces; `AccessBlocked` pentru `PLAN_FEATURE_REQUIRED`; /admin/settings cu randuri de plan + formular „Plan nou”; /admin/payments si KPI pe plan; dashboard firma si pagina preluarii cu `companyCan()`; `TierBadge` cu eticheta.
  - DB: `20261001060000_plans_as_data` (expand blue/green): `subscription_plans.code` NOT NULL UNIQUE (backfill din `tier`), `name_ro` / `name_en`, `tier` NULLABLE (legacy); tabel nou `subscription_plan_features` (PK `plan_id, feature_key`, FK CASCADE, `limit_value` pentru cote viitoare) cu backfill `MARKETPLACE_ACCESS = true` pe SILVER/GOLD/PLATINUM. Rollback in comentariul migrarii. Preturi 149/399/899 neschimbate.
  - Realtime: fara evenimente noi (entitlements se reincarca prin invalidarea existenta `['billing']`).
  - Securitate (security-close, peste diff-ul pachetului): endpointurile noi `POST /admin/plans` / `PATCH /admin/plans/:id` sub `@Roles(ADMIN)` la nivel de controller, DTO class-validator (whitelist + forbidNonWhitelisted; `code` imutabil pe PATCH), `ParseUUIDPipe`; `GET /billing/entitlements` sub `@Roles(COMPANY_USER)` + `CompanyApprovedGuard`, scoped pe `companyContext.companyId` (fara `:id` in ruta), doar citire. Nicio mutatie noua de bani: cumpararea ramane cu `@Idempotent({ transactional: true })`, iar blocarea planului gratuit se face inainte de orice scriere. Fara loguri noi, fara PII, fara secrete in cod/seed/migrare; erorile noi au coduri in ERROR_CODES + docs/ERROR_CODES.md. Soft-delete: nu se aplica (planurile nu au `deleted_at`; dezactivare prin `is_active`). Fara localStorage nou in FE. Paritate i18n RO/EN 3579/3579, 0 chei lipsa. **Fix la inchidere**: limita tehnica INT4 (`@Max(2_147_483_647)` + zod `.max`) pe `features.*.limit`, `marketplaceGatingDelayMin` (create + PATCH) si `includedCredits` (PATCH) — inainte o valoare peste INT4 trecea validarea si dadea 500 din Postgres; test nou in `admin-plans.spec.ts`.
  - Audit: `PLAN_CREATED` / `PLAN_UPDATED` (`critical-audit-metadata.spec`); snapshot cu `code`, `features`, `isFree` — numele planurilor (text liber) raman afara; `trial_plan` intra doar ca cod de plan (`PLAN_CODE_RE`) prin `SETTING_UPDATED`.
- **Fisiere**: vezi lista din intrarea E-admin-platform-58 de mai jos; la verificare: `apps/frontend/src/app/[locale]/marketplace/wallet/page.tsx` (linia de gating ascunsa pe planurile fara `MARKETPLACE_ACCESS`); la inchidere: `apps/backend/src/modules/admin/dto/admin.dto.ts`, `packages/shared/src/admin.schemas.ts`, `apps/backend/src/modules/admin/admin-plans.spec.ts`, `CHANGELOG.md`.
- **Plan de test manual**:
  - owner.a pe /ro/marketplace/wallet: SILVER 180,29 / GOLD 482,79 / PLATINUM 1.087,79 lei (149/399/899 + TVA 21%), „Acces la marketplace: inclus în planul tău”; `GET /billing/entitlements` → GOLD cu `MARKETPLACE_ACCESS` activ.
  - admin pe /ro/admin/settings: „Plan nou” TEST_FREE la 0 lei fara acces → insigna „Gratuit”; acelasi cod a doua oara → „Există deja un plan cu acest cod.”; `PATCH /admin/plans/:id` cu `features.MARKETPLACE_ACCESS.limit = 3000000000` → 400.
  - /ro/admin/payments: acorda TEST_FREE (1 zi) unei firme de test → /marketplace arata ecranul `PLAN_FEATURE_REQUIRED`; portofelul nu are buton de cumparare pe planul gratuit si nu afiseaza „Acces imediat la cererile noi”; bifeaza accesul pe plan → marketplace se incarca. Readu abonamentul firmei si sterge planul de test.
  - `PUT /admin/settings/trial_plan` cu `NOPE` → 400 `PLAN_NOT_FOUND`; cu `GOLD` → 200.
  - /en pe aceleasi pagini: fara chei lipsa.
- **Checklist de acceptare**:
  - [x] `pnpm -F shared build && pnpm -F shared test` (14 fisiere / 394 teste)
  - [x] Migrarea aplicata local, `code = tier` pe cele 3 planuri, 3 randuri `MARKETPLACE_ACCESS = true`, `prisma migrate diff` fara drift
  - [x] Jest backend complet 101 suite / 1225 teste (verificator); la inchidere suitele admin/entitlements/garzi/audit 15 / 215 teste verzi (+1 test INT4 in `admin-plans.spec.ts`)
  - [x] `pnpm typecheck` si `pnpm lint` verzi (rulate din nou la inchidere); Vitest FE 50 fisiere / 338 teste
  - [x] Paritate i18n RO/EN 3579/3579, 0 chei lipsa
  - [x] `GET /billing/entitlements` GOLD + `GET /marketplace/requests` 200; `GET /billing/plans` 180,29 / 482,79 / 1087,79
  - [x] `POST /admin/plans` → 201 `isFree`; repetat → 409 `PLAN_CODE_TAKEN`; owner → 403; `code` pe PATCH → 400; id non-uuid → 400
  - [x] Plan fara acces → 403 `PLAN_FEATURE_REQUIRED`; dupa bifa → 200; `POST /billing/subscription/purchase` pe plan de 0 lei → 409 `PLAN_NOT_PURCHASABLE`, 0 comenzi
  - [x] `trial_plan` NOPE → 400 `PLAN_NOT_FOUND`; GOLD → 200
  - [x] claim_cost_min/max (E-company-21) verificat live; `requests.credit_cost` existente neschimbate; setari readuse la 1/0
  - [x] UI /ro si /en: /admin/settings (planuri), /marketplace/wallet, ecranul `PLAN_FEATURE_REQUIRED`
  - [x] Date demo readuse (abonamentul firmei A pe GOLD, TEST_FREE sters, `trial_plan=GOLD`)
  - [x] docs/07 neatins de acest sprint
  - [ ] EMPLOYEE_MANAGED → 403 pe `GET /billing/orders` live (parola demo a angajatilor nu e cea locala standard; acoperit de `company-manager.guard.spec` + `company-permissions.test`)
  - [ ] Test live pe o a doua firma demo (rulat pe firma A cu snapshot + restaurare)
  - [ ] Pasul PO: grila de preturi in docs/07 (blocat — DECIZIE NECESARA)
- **DECIZIE NECESARA (PO)**:
  - Grila „runda 4” (Start 0 / Ofertare 299 / Prioritate 599, cost claim 3..25) e finala? Daca da, PO inlocuieste explicit Î7, Î8, D-v6-5 si D-L0-2 in docs/07. Pana atunci preturile raman 149/399/899.
  - Facturare recurenta Stripe (mode subscription / anual) in locul platii unice + 30 de zile (ar inlocui D-L0-1 / D-L0-2)?
  - Cum ajunge o firma pe planul gratuit: la aprobare (in locul trialului Gold), automat cand nu are abonament (plan implicit) sau doar prin grant admin? Azi: grant admin sau `trial_plan`.
  - Planul gratuit da acces la marketplace + preluari (plata doar cu credite) sau e limitat? Azi: bifa per plan, implicit NU pentru planurile noi.
  - Ce functii de unealta (Ofertare Rapida, citiri AI / luna) intra in fiecare plan si cu ce limite?
  - Valorile `claim_cost_min` / `claim_cost_max` (propunerea 3..25) — deschise din E-company-21.
  - Grant admin al unui plan mai ieftin/gratuit peste un abonament platit activ: `grantOrExtend` muta `planId` si aduna zile, deci timpul platit ramas devine timp pe planul gratuit fara avertizare (si invers la cumpararea unui plan platit de pe planul gratuit). Comportament anterior sprintului; minim, UI-ul admin ar trebui sa avertizeze — PO decide regula.
- **Ramas**:
  - Release separat (contract): `DROP COLUMN tier` + `DROP TYPE subscription_plan_tier`, dupa ce niciun slot blue/green nu mai ruleaza imaginea veche.
  - Blue/green: cat timp ruleaza imaginea de rollback, un plan nou (tier NULL) e citit de cod vechi care presupune enum non-null, iar `MARKETPLACE_ACCESS=false` e ignorat — nu se creeaza planuri noi pana cand ambele sloturi ruleaza imaginea noua.
  - Verificarile de rol inline care nu se mapeaza 1:1 pe o permisiune (retrageri, livrare, caz proiect, acces la contacte, destinatari notificari, reguli OWNER) raman neschimbate — lista in intrarea pachetului.
  - Igiena date (anterioara): randuri `subscriptions` cu status ACTIVE dar expirate (firmele A, B, C, G, H); comportamentul e corect prin `activeSubscriptionWhere` (filtreaza pe `expiresAt`), dar jobul de expirare nu comuta statusul — de verificat separat.
  - Paneul de browser local a ramas logat ca admin@demo.ro; sesiunile vechi ale owner.a au fost revocate de logarile curl (asteptat).

- **Obiectiv**: planurile si functiile lor devin date editabile din Admin, cu un singur serviciu de entitlements, FARA a schimba vreun pret, numar de credite, durata sau regula. Grila „runda 4” (Start 0 / Ofertare 299 / Prioritate 599, cost claim 3..25, facturare recurenta) NU e in docs/07 → ramane DECIZIE NECESARA; docs/07 nu a fost atins.
- **Impact**:
  - DB: `20261001060000_plans_as_data` (expand blue/green): `subscription_plans.code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE` (backfill din `tier`), `name_ro` / `name_en TEXT NULL`, `tier` devine NULLABLE (legacy; DROP in release separat); tabel nou `subscription_plan_features` (PK `plan_id, feature_key`, FK CASCADE, `enabled`, `limit_value`, `updated_at`) cu backfill `MARKETPLACE_ACCESS = true` pe SILVER/GOLD/PLATINUM. Rollback + nota blue/green (imaginea veche ignora functiile) in comentariul migrarii. Seed: upsert dupa `code` (+ rand `MARKETPLACE_ACCESS`, create-only); seed-demo cauta planurile dupa `code`. Preturi/credite/trial neschimbate.
  - Shared: `plan-features.ts` (`PLAN_FEATURE_KEYS = ['MARKETPLACE_ACCESS']`, `PLAN_FEATURE_DEFAULTS`, `PLAN_CODE_RE`, `resolvePlanFeatures`), `company-permissions.ts` (`COMPANY_PERMISSIONS`, `COMPANY_ROLE_PERMISSIONS`, `can()`), `EntitlementsDto`, `AdminPlanDto` / `AdminCreditPackageDto` / `AdminThresholdDto`, `createPlanSchema` (features obligatoriu), `updatePlanSchema` (pret >= 0, nume, features partial, fara `code`), `AdminKpiDto.activeSubscriptionsByPlan`, `SubscriptionPlanDto` + `CreditPackageDto` cu `vatRate/vatRon/totalRon` (+ `isFree`, nume, `features` pe plan), `tier`/`planTier` = `PlanCode` (acelasi nume de camp; chitantele de idempotenta raman valide), `CompanyDto.permissions`, coduri noi `PLAN_FEATURE_REQUIRED` (403), `PLAN_NOT_PURCHASABLE` (409), `PLAN_CODE_TAKEN` (409). `SUBSCRIPTION_PLAN_TIERS` ramane doar lista codurilor legacy (paritate enum Prisma 0 diferente).
  - Backend: modul nou `modules/entitlements` (`EntitlementsService.forCompany/has/toDto`, `activeSubscriptionWhere()` = SINGURUL predicat „abonament activ”, `PlanFeatureGuard` + `@RequirePlanFeature`) importat de billing/admin/claims/marketplace. `SubscriptionActiveGuard` citeste prin entitlements: fara abonament 403 `SUBSCRIPTION_INACTIVE` (neschimbat), plan fara `MARKETPLACE_ACCESS` 403 `PLAN_FEATURE_REQUIRED`, altfel `subscriptionId` + `gatingDelayMinutes` + `entitlements` pe context. Predicatul inlocuit in `SubscriptionsService.grantOrExtend/getActive`, `SubscriptionExpiryProcessor` (findMany; re-verificarea exacta pe `expiresAt` ramane) si KPI admin (+ `groupBy planId` → coduri). `GET /billing/entitlements` (toata echipa, GET fara scrieri). `billing/vat.ts` (`buildAmounts` mutat identic din PaymentsService + `priceWithVat`); `listPackages` / `listPlans` intorc TVA si total din `vat_rate`. `purchaseSubscription` pe plan de 0 lei → 409 `PLAN_NOT_PURCHASABLE` inainte de comanda si de Stripe. `tier` → `code` in payments / bank-transfer / e-invoice-export / invoice-pdf / subscriptions (textul facturii si descrierea Stripe identice pentru planurile vechi). `startTrialIfEnabled` cauta planul dupa `code`. Admin: `POST /admin/plans` (`@Audit PLAN_CREATED`, P2002 → 409 `PLAN_CODE_TAKEN`, runTx write), `PATCH /admin/plans/:id` cu `ParseUUIDPipe`, pret >= 0, nume, upsert pe functii; `trial_plan` validat (cod `PLAN_CODE_RE` al unui plan ACTIV, altfel 400 `PLAN_NOT_FOUND`); DTO-uri tipate pentru pachete/praguri. Audit snapshot: `code`, `features` + cheile din registru, `enabled`, `isFree`, `limit`/`limitValue`; `trial_plan` acceptat dupa `PLAN_CODE_RE` (numele planurilor raman afara — text liber). Permisiuni firma: `CompanyManagerGuard` = `can(role, 'BILLING_MANAGE')`, `CompaniesService.requireManager(userId, permission)` (profil/locatii/portofoliu = `COMPANY_PROFILE_EDIT`, echipa = `MEMBERS_MANAGE`), `billingEmail` vizibil cu `BILLING_MANAGE`, „De facut acum” cu `CLAIMS_ASSIGN`, `ClaimsService.assign` = `CLAIMS_ASSIGN`; `CompanyDto.permissions`.

## Sprint 23 (plan 2026-09-28): Invoice and payment correctness — 2026-09-29
- **Obiectiv**: inchiderea sprintului care face banii si facturile corecte pentru firme: cifre corecte in portofel si pe dashboard + angajatii fara cumparare (E-company-22), facturi corecte fiscal si marcate ca platite (E-company-19), plata prin transfer bancar completa + emailuri de facturare doar la OWNER/MANAGER (E-company-24). Detaliile per pachet sunt in cele trei intrari de mai jos.
- **Impact**:
  - Backend: `dashboardStats` → `wallet.available = balance` (ca `GET /billing/wallet`); Stripe Checkout cu `locale` ro/en din `users.language_preference` + descriere RO/EN; factura PDF cu date Europe/Bucharest, Client cu `Cod TVA RO…`/`CUI`, coloane Nr. crt./U.M./Cant./Pret unitar, „Total factura”, „Achitat” + „Rest de plata 0,00 RON”, IBAN doar la transfer, `filename="factura-{serie}-{numar}.pdf"`; `buyer_snapshot` v2 cu `isVatPayer`; perioada serviciului scrisa la `confirm()`; date Bucharest si in PDF-ul ofertei si in emailul `company.suspended`. Transfer bancar: `payment.transfer_instructions` (IBAN + suma + „Comanda {REF}” + proforma PDF fara numar fiscal), `GET /billing/orders/:id/proforma`, `POST /billing/orders/:id/mark-paid`, sweep orar `bank-transfer-expiry` (reminder + anulare, oprit implicit 0/0); `payment.confirmed` si `subscription.expiring` doar spre OWNER/MANAGER, factura atasata la confirmare; `MailService` cu atasamente; `companies.billing_email` (copie pentru contabil o data per eveniment).
  - Frontend: portofel cu Disponibile / Rezervate in preluari / Total detinut, vizualizare read-only pentru angajati (fara cereri la `/billing/orders` si `/billing/payment-instructions`), `AccessBlocked` pentru firme suspendate/neaprobate, erori de cumparare in dialog, bani printr-un helper unic (`Common.money`: lei RO / RON EN, 2 zecimale), butoane Copiaza (IBAN fara spatii, suma, referinta), `apiBlob()` cu refresh la 401 + `saveBlob()` pentru descarcari, „Descarca proforma”, „Am platit”, insigna „Plata semnalata” (si in /admin/payments), email de facturare pe profilul firmei, insigna „Platitoare de TVA”, „Perioada de proba” in RO.

Ambele strict aditive, rollback in comentarii; `stripe_checkout_attempt` primeste `locale` in JSONB (fara DDL). docs/04 actualizat.

  - Securitate (security-close): endpointurile noi `GET /billing/orders/:id/proforma` si `POST /billing/orders/:id/mark-paid` stau sub `@Roles(COMPANY_USER)` + `CompanyApprovedGuard` + `CompanyManagerGuard` (angajat → 403), cu `ParseUUIDPipe` si ownership pe `companyId` (alta firma → 404, re-verificat sub `FOR UPDATE` in `markPaid`); `GET /admin/payments/:id/invoice` sub `@Roles(ADMIN)`. `mark-paid` fara Idempotency-Key, dar idempotent pe stare (`payment_claimed_at` setat o data) + cheia outbox (nu muta bani); cumpararile raman cu `@Idempotent({ transactional: true })`. `PATCH /companies/me`: DTO class-validator (`@IsBoolean isVatPayer`, `@IsEmail @MaxLength(254) billingEmail`), `requireManager`, `billingEmail` intors doar pentru OWNER/MANAGER. `Content-Disposition` construit doar din seria curatata `[A-Za-z0-9_-]` + numar / ref hex. Emailurile noi escapeaza IBAN/CUI/numele firmei (`esc`); logurile au doar id-uri si `safeErrorSummary` (fara adrese de email). Destinatarii de facturare exclud conturile sterse/blocate. Fara secrete noi in cod/seed/migrari. ## Sprint 23 (plan 2026-09-28): E-company-24 — plata prin transfer bancar completa + emailuri de facturare doar la OWNER/MANAGER — 2026-09-30
- **Obiectiv**: cand Stripe e oprit (D-L0-1), firma primeste pe email datele de plata (IBAN, suma, „Comanda {REF}”) + factura proforma PDF, poate descarca proforma din portofel si poate apasa „Am plătit”; confirmarea platii vine cu factura atasata; emailurile si clopotelul de facturare (`payment.confirmed`, `subscription.expiring`, transfer) ajung doar la OWNER/MANAGER; reminder + anulare automata a comenzilor neplatite (oprite pana la decizia PO); email de facturare optional al firmei (contabil) care primeste documentele.
- **Impact**:
  - DB: `20261001050000_bank_transfer_billing_emails` (strict aditiva): `mock_billing_orders.payment_claimed_at` / `transfer_reminder_sent_at`, `companies.billing_email VARCHAR(254)`, `domain_event_outbox.billing_contact_emailed_at`; date `system_settings` `bank_transfer_reminder_days='0'`, `bank_transfer_expiry_days='0'` (si in seed.ts / `SETTING_DEFAULTS`). docs/04 actualizat (Actualizare, tabel 5.3, §5.4). Rollback in comentariul migrarii.
  - Backend billing: `billing-recipients.ts` (`billingManagerIds`: OWNER/MANAGER activi) pentru `payment.confirmed` si `subscription.expiring`; `createPendingOrder` (Stripe oprit) scrie si `payment.transfer_instructions:{orderId}` spre OWNER/MANAGER; `invoice-pdf.service`: `INVOICE_CSS` extras (factura identica byte cu byte), `buildProformaHtml` / `orderRef` / `proformaFileName`, `generateProforma` / `proformaForOrder` / `invoiceForOrder`; `BillingDocumentsService` (rehidrateaza suma/IBAN/ref + PDF memorat 10 min, randare esuata = email fara atasament); `BankTransferService` (`markPaid`, `sweep`); endpointuri noi `GET /billing/orders/:id/proforma` si `POST /billing/orders/:id/mark-paid` (`@Audit PAYMENT_MARKED_PAID`, `CompanyManagerGuard`); job `bank-transfer-expiry` pe coada `subscription-expiry` (orar `15 * * * *` + rulare la boot). DTO: `BillingOrderDto.paymentClaimedAt/transferExpiresAt`, `AdminBillingOrderItemDto.paymentClaimedAt`.

- **Obiectiv**: factura PDF arata data emiterii in Europe/Bucharest, codul de TVA al clientului platitor, coloanele U.M. / Cant. / Pret unitar, perioada abonamentului, „Total factură” + „Achitat … / Rest de plată 0,00 RON”, IBAN doar la transfer bancar; fisierul se numeste `factura-{serie}-{numar}.pdf` si se descarca prin sesiune (refresh la 401), nu printr-un `<a href>` care pica dupa expirarea access token-ului.

Registrul CSV: `buyer_cui` = codul tiparit pe factura. `GET billing/orders/:id/invoice` si `GET admin/payments/:id/invoice`: `Content-Disposition` per factura.
  - Firme: onboarding cu CUI `RO…` → `isVatPayer = true` (cui salvat tot fara prefix; unicitatea si blocajul de reaplicare neschimbate); `PATCH /companies/me` accepta `isVatPayer` si e auditat `COMPANY_PROFILE_UPDATED` (snapshot doar id/status/isVatPayer/locatii — numele si adresa raman afara); `CompanyDto.isVatPayer`.

  - Realtime: neschimbat. Securitate: fara endpoint nou; seria din numele fisierului e curatata (`[A-Za-z0-9_-]`) inainte de header.

## Sprint 22 (plan 2026-09-28): Settle-once credits and credit rules — 2026-09-29
- **Obiectiv**: inchiderea sprintului care face ca fiecare rezervare de credite sa se deconteze exact o data (E-admin-platform-15: o singura cale `ClaimSettlementService.settle()`, plase DB, verificare zilnica ledger vs portofel, LIVE-11 reparat) si muta regulile costului unei preluari in setari (E-company-21: `credit_value_ron`, `claim_cost_min`, `claim_cost_max`, implicit = comportamentul de azi). Detaliile per pachet sunt in cele doua intrari de mai jos.
- **Impact**:
  - Backend: `settle()` = tranzitie conditionata a slotului + oferte/chat dupa mod + ledger-ul decide (`REFUNDED` / `CONSUMED` / `SKIPPED_NO_RESERVE` / `SKIPPED_ALREADY_SETTLED` / `SKIPPED_WALLET_DRIFT`); `refund`/`consume` conditionate pe `reserved >= amount`; toti apelantii (claims, quotes, requests, fulfillment) trec prin `settle()`; `refunded` urmeaza rezultatul real. `SizingService` + preview admin folosesc `creditCostFromBaseScore(baseScore, rules)` din shared. `PUT /admin/settings/:key` valideaza cele 3 chei noi + verificarea min/max (`CLAIM_COST_RANGE`). `GET /legal/firm-rules` intoarce `claims.claimCost`.
  - Frontend: clopotel `credits.ledger_mismatch` (doar admini), `ClaimCostNote` pe Admin → Setari si /admin/sizing, nota „fara abonament activ” in portofel, propozitia de interval pe /firm-rules doar cand exista o limita.
  - DB: `20261001010000_credit_settlement_unique` (2 indexuri unice partiale pe `credit_transactions.claim_slot_id`), `20261001011000_wallet_check_validate` (VALIDATE pe `*_nonneg`), `20261001020000_claim_cost_settings` (doar date, 3 randuri `system_settings`). Fara schimbare de model/enum Prisma.
  - Realtime: eveniment nou `credits.ledger_mismatch` (outbox, o data pe zi, destinatari = adminii activi); `use-socket` il asculta.
  - Securitate (security-close): endpoint nou unic `GET /admin/credits/ledger-check` sub `@Roles(ADMIN)` la nivel de controller (owner → 403, anonim → 401), doar citiri, SQL brut numai `Prisma.sql` fara input utilizator; nicio mutatie de bani noua expusa pe HTTP (decontarea ruleaza in tranzactiile existente, idempotenta prin biletul conditionat + indexurile unice); logurile si payload-ul outbox au doar id-uri/sume/numaratori (fara PII); fara secrete in cod/migrari; erorile noi folosesc `VALIDATION_ERROR` din ERROR_CODES; interogarea adminilor filtreaza `deletedAt: null`; paritate i18n RO/EN 3514/3514 chei.
  - Audit: `SETTING_UPDATED` (existent) acopera cele 3 chei noi, cu valori numerice in allowlist-ul `audit-snapshot`; raportul ledger-check e citire (fara audit).
- **Fisiere**: vezi listele din intrarile E-admin-platform-15 si E-company-21 de mai jos; la inchidere: `apps/frontend/src/messages/en.json` (`Admin.settings.creditCostRange`: `{max}` cu plural ICU, ca in RO).
- **Plan de test manual**:
  - Admin local: `GET /api/v1/admin/credits/ledger-check` → `walletMismatches: []`, `unsettledTerminalSlots: []`; acelasi apel ca owner.a → 403, fara sesiune → 401.
  - Redis: `ZCARD bull:sla-breach:failed` = 0; /admin/jobs fara esecuri sla-breach; sloturile demo fara RESERVE ajung SLA_EXPIRED cu `claim.withdrawn` (`refunded=false`).
  - Admin → Setari: `claim_cost_max=25` → 200 si nota „intre 1 si 25 de credite” (RO) / „between 1 and 25 credits” (EN); `claim_cost_min=30` → 400 `CLAIM_COST_RANGE`; `credit_value_ron=0` → 400; restaurare `claim_cost_max=0`.
  - /ro/firm-rules: fara propozitia de cost cu implicitele; cu plafon setat apare intervalul (cache public 5 min).
  - Portofel: owner.a (GOLD activ) fara nota; o firma fara abonament activ vede nota ambra, cumpararea ramane permisa.
- **Checklist de acceptare**:
  - [x] `pnpm typecheck` si `pnpm lint` verzi la inchidere (exit 0)
  - [x] Jest backend complet verde (90 suite / 1083 teste dupa ambele pachete), Vitest FE 48 fisiere / 327 teste, shared 375/375; `legal-messages.test` 11/11 dupa fixul EN
  - [x] Integrare Postgres local `--runInBand`: `credit-settlement` 12/12 (h/i/j), `claims.concurrency` 5/5, `quotes.concurrency` 8/8
  - [x] `credits.refund/consume` apelate doar din `billing/claim-settlement.service.ts`; regula ESLint pica pe orice alt apel
  - [x] Indexurile unice partiale exista; ambele CHECK-uri `*_nonneg` au `convalidated = t`; `system_settings` = 1000 / 1 / 0
  - [x] LIVE-11: cele 4 sloturi SLA demo = SLA_EXPIRED, 0 joburi esuate sla-breach / claim-assign / reconciliation
  - [x] Ledger-check: admin 200 curat, owner 403, anonim 401; job `ledger-check` programat 03:40 Europe/Bucharest
  - [x] Validarea setarilor de cost (200 / 400 `CLAIM_COST_RANGE` / 400) si preview-ul cu plafon verificate live
  - [x] Paritate chei i18n RO/EN (0 chei lipsa in ambele sensuri)

- **DECIZIE NECESARA (PO)**:
  - Politica pentru portofel deviat (RESERVE exista, dar `reserved` e sub suma): azi fara eliberare partiala si fara corectie automata; tranzitia se comite, nu se scrie rand in ledger, verificarea zilnica alerteaza. Ramane de decis: reparare manuala/automata si cine absoarbe diferenta.
  - Valorile `claim_cost_min` / `claim_cost_max` (propunerea 3..25 nu e in docs/03 sau docs/07); implicit 1 / 0 (fara plafon), setabile din Admin fara deploy.
  - Expirarea creditelor (docs/03 4.16): neimplementata; pastrare (tip EXPIRE + job + avertizari + clauza in Termeni) / post-MVP / renuntare.
- **Ramas**:
  - Pre-verificarile pe prod inainte de migrarile `20261001010000` / `20261001011000` (prod cu `RUN_MIGRATIONS=false`, dupa backup).
  - `prisma generate` dupa oprirea dev serverului.
  - Alerta admin pentru joburi BullMQ esuate si scriptul de reparare a portofelelor deviate (in afara pachetului; repararea depinde de decizia PO).
  - `/admin/jobs` afiseaza `failedReason` brut (text de eroare Prisma cu cale locala) — preexistent, doar pentru admin, dar contrazice regula E-admin-platform-8 fara erori brute; 2 esecuri vechi `domain-events` din 2026-09-29 (DB indisponibila) pot fi reincercate/sterse.
  - Exceptia demo din verificarea zilnica (toti membrii `@demo.ro`, inclusiv utilizatori soft-deleted) sare doar identitatea balance; local toate cele 8 portofele sunt demo, deci identitatea balance e exersata doar de testul de integrare (j).
  - Verificarea min/max la salvarea setarilor nu e tranzactionala (doi admini simultan pot lasa max < min; `resolveClaimCostRules` ridica max la min la citire).
  - `settle()` citeste ledger-ul in tranzactiile Serializable: de urmarit dupa deploy rata de reincercari SSI / 409 `CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION`.
  - Concurrency suites paralele pe aceeasi DB locala pica prin contentie (trec cu `--runInBand`, ca in CI).
  - `GET /legal/firm-rules` cu `Cache-Control: public, max-age=300`: intervalul vechi poate aparea pana la 5 minute.

## Sprint 22 / E-company-21: Regulile costului unei preluari in setari (credite) — 2026-10-01
- **Obiectiv**: valoarea unui credit si intervalul costului unei preluari devin configurabile din Admin fara deploy (o singura formula in shared), adminul le vede langa ponderile de scoring, portofelul spune ca creditele se folosesc doar cu abonament activ, pagina publica arata intervalul doar cand exista o limita. Implicitele = comportamentul de dinainte (1 credit = 1.000 lei din bugetul minim, minim 1, fara plafon).
- **Module / tabele**: shared (`request.schemas`, `legal`, comentariu `enums`), BE sizing (`SizingService.compute` / `computeSizing`), admin (`SETTING_VALUE_RULES` + verificare min/max, `AdminSizingService.preview`), common settings (`SETTING_DEFAULTS`), audit (`NUMERIC_SETTINGS`), legal (`getFirmRules`); FE admin (Setari + Marime proiect), portofel firma, pagina /firm-rules. Tabele: `system_settings` (3 randuri noi, doar date); `requests.credit_cost` scris la publicare/editare cu formula noua (fara schimbare de schema); `project_sizing_config` / `project_size_thresholds` doar citite.
- **Impact**:
  - Shared: `CLAIM_COST_SETTING_KEYS`, `ClaimCostRules`, `CLAIM_COST_DEFAULTS`, `CLAIM_COST_LIMITS`, `resolveClaimCostRules(raw)` (invalid/lipsa → implicit; max 0/invalid → null; max < min → min), `creditCostFromBaseScore(baseScore, rules = implicite)` = `min(max(min, ceil(baseScore*1000 / creditValueRon)), max|inf)`; `FirmRulesDto.claims.claimCost = { min, max | null }`.
  - Backend: `SizingService.compute` citeste cele 3 chei in acelasi `Promise.all` (in afara tranzactiei de publish/edit, +1 query paralel); previzualizarea `/admin/sizing/preview` aplica aceleasi reguli pe ambele calcule (curent/propus). `PUT /admin/settings/:key`: `credit_value_ron` 1..1.000.000, `claim_cost_min` 1..1000, `claim_cost_max` 0 sau 1..1000; plafon > 0 sub minim → 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` (`details[].reason = CLAIM_COST_RANGE`). Auditul `SETTING_UPDATED` pastreaza valorile (chei numerice). `GET /legal/firm-rules` intoarce `claims.claimCost`.
  - Frontend: componenta noua `admin/_components/claim-cost-note.tsx` (valoarea creditului + intervalul, din `useSettings`) pe Admin → Setari → Praguri si pe /admin/sizing; portofel: nota ambra sub „Cumpara credite” cand firma nu are abonament activ (cumpararea ramane permisa); /firm-rules: propozitia cu intervalul doar cand `claim_cost_max > 0` sau `claim_cost_min > 1`. i18n RO/EN: `Admin.settings.creditCostNote` (rescris, cu `{creditValue}`), `Admin.settings.creditCostRange`, `Admin.settings.creditCostNoMax`, `Billing.noSubscriptionCreditsNote`, `Legal.firmRules.sections.credits.costRange`, `Legal.firmRules.sections.credits.costMin` (+5 chei noi per limba). `Legal.terms.*` neatins.
  - DB: migrarea de date `20261001020000_claim_cost_settings` (3× `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`: `credit_value_ron=1000`, `claim_cost_min=1`, `claim_cost_max=0`); seed.ts are aceleasi chei. Sigura pe ambele sloturi blue/green (slotul vechi ignora cheile). Rollback: redeploy imaginea anterioara, apoi `DELETE FROM system_settings WHERE key IN ('credit_value_ron','claim_cost_min','claim_cost_max');`.
  - Schimbarea setarilor re-evalueaza DOAR cererile publicate sau editate dupa salvare; `requests.credit_cost` existent si `claim_slots.claim_cost_credits_snapshot` nu se recalculeaza (IMPACT credite §6). Niciun cod de ledger/decontare atins.
- **Fisiere**: shared `src/{request.schemas, request.schemas.test, legal, enums}.ts`; backend `prisma/{seed.ts, migrations/20261001020000_claim_cost_settings/migration.sql}`, `src/common/settings/setting-defaults.ts`, `src/modules/sizing/sizing.service(.spec).ts`, `src/modules/admin/{admin-sizing.service(.spec), admin.service, admin-settings-validation.spec}.ts`, `src/modules/audit/audit-snapshot.ts`, `src/modules/legal/{legal.constants, legal.service(.spec)}.ts`; frontend `app/[locale]/admin/_components/claim-cost-note.tsx` (NOU), `app/[locale]/admin/{settings,sizing}/page.tsx`, `app/[locale]/marketplace/wallet/page.tsx`, `app/[locale]/_components/firm-rules-article(.test).tsx`, `messages/{ro,en}.json`, `messages/legal-messages.test.ts` (allowlist ICU +`min`/`max`); docs `03-business-rules.md` (note 4.5 / 4.8 / 4.16), `04-data-model.md` (Actualizare + §5.4).

## Sprint 22 / E-admin-platform-15: Fiecare rezervare de credite se deconteaza exact o data — 2026-10-01
- **Obiectiv**: o singura cale de decontare (`ClaimSettlementService.settle()`) pentru toate inchiderile de slot care misca credite, cu ledger-ul ca sursa de adevar; joburile SLA / neatribuire nu mai pica pe veci pe CHECK-ul `reserved >= 0` (LIVE-11: sloturi demo/vechi fara RESERVE); plase DB (indexuri unice partiale + CHECK-uri VALIDATE); verificare zilnica ledger vs portofel cu alerta la admini.
- **Module / tabele**: billing (`CreditsService`, nou `ClaimSettlementService`, nou `LedgerCheckService`), claims (claim-assign, sla-breach, withdrawals, offer-expiry-settlement, reconciliation; `claims/settlement.ts` sters), quotes (accept), requests (stergere), fulfillment (completare, inchidere proiect), admin (endpoint nou), event-bus (eveniment nou), shared (motive de credit), FE (clopotel + socket + i18n). Tabele: `credit_transactions` (2 indexuri SQL-only), `company_credit_wallets` (VALIDATE), `claim_withdrawals.refunded` (urmeaza rezultatul real), `domain_event_outbox`/`notifications` (tip nou `credits.ledger_mismatch`).
- **Impact**:
  - Backend: `settle(tx, args)` = updateMany conditionat (bilet) → oferte/chat dupa mod (`WITHDRAWN`/`SUPERSEDED`/`CHAT_READ_ONLY`/`KEEP`) → citire ledger: fara RESERVE → `SKIPPED_NO_RESERVE`, decontare existenta → `SKIPPED_ALREADY_SETTLED`, portofel sub suma → `SKIPPED_WALLET_DRIFT`, altfel exact un REFUND/CONSUME pe suma RESERVE-ului. Tranzitia de status se comite in toate cazurile. `refund`/`consume` fac update conditionat pe `reserved >= amount` si intorc `false` fara rand in ledger. `COMPLETED` nu primeste `withdrawnAt`. Toti cei 9 apelanti (7 fisiere) + cele 2 cai E-admin-platform-3 trec prin `settle()`; `refunded` din payload-urile `claim.withdrawn` / `claim_withdrawals` vine din rezultat (nu din intentie). Rezultatele de business (IMPACT credite pct. 3) neschimbate.
  - ESLint: `credits.refund/consume` interzis in afara `billing/claim-settlement.service.ts` (specurile raman exceptate).
  - Shared: `CREDIT_GRANT_REASONS`, `CREDIT_RESERVE_REASONS`, `CREDIT_SETTLEMENT_REASONS`, `CREDIT_REASONS` + tipuri (exact stringurile scrise azi; coloana ramane text).
  - Verificare zilnica: job `ledger-check` pe coada `reconciliation` (`40 3 * * *`, Europe/Bucharest) + `startup-ledger-check` la boot; identitati portofel (reserved pentru toate firmele, balance doar non-demo = firme cu TOTI membrii `@demo.ro`), sloturi terminale cu RESERVE nedecontat, sloturi deschise deja decontate. Neconcordanta → `logger.error` (doar numaratori) + o intentie outbox pe zi `credits.ledger_mismatch:<YYYY-MM-DD>` catre adminii activi (clopotel, fara email). `GET /admin/credits/ledger-check` (ADMIN, doar citire, fara audit/alerta).
  - Frontend: clopotel `credits.ledger_mismatch` (AlertTriangle → `/admin/jobs`), `use-socket` asculta evenimentul; i18n +1 cheie RO/EN (`Notifications.type.credits.ledger_mismatch`).
  - DB: migrari SQL-only `20261001010000_credit_settlement_unique` (2 indexuri unice partiale) si `20261001011000_wallet_check_validate` (VALIDATE pe ambele CHECK-uri). Aplicate local. Rollback in headerul fiecarei migrari (DROP INDEX; VALIDATE nu necesita rollback).
  - Securitate: endpoint nou doar GET sub `@Roles(ADMIN)` (owner → 403, anonim → 401); SQL brut doar `Prisma.sql` fara input utilizator; loguri doar cu id-uri/sume/numaratori; payload outbox doar numaratori.
- **Operator — pre-verificari pe prod INAINTE de migrari** (toate trebuie 0 randuri; local 0 la 2026-09-30):
  - `SELECT claim_slot_id, count(*) FROM credit_transactions WHERE type IN ('REFUND','CONSUME') AND claim_slot_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY 1 HAVING count(*) > 1;`
  - `SELECT claim_slot_id, count(*) FROM credit_transactions WHERE type = 'RESERVE' AND claim_slot_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY 1 HAVING count(*) > 1;`
  - `SELECT company_id FROM company_credit_wallets WHERE balance < 0 OR reserved < 0;`
  - Prod ruleaza cu `RUN_MIGRATIONS=false` (OPS1-23): migrarile se aplica o data, dupa backup.
- **Fisiere**: shared `src/enums.ts`; backend `modules/billing/{credits.service(.spec), claim-settlement.service(.spec) NOU, ledger-check.service(.spec) NOU, billing.module}.ts`, `modules/claims/{claim-assign.processor, sla-breach.processor, withdrawals.service(.spec), offer-expiry-settlement.processor(.spec), reconciliation.processor, lifecycle-processors.spec, credit-settlement.integration.spec}.ts` (sterse `settlement.ts` + `settlement.spec.ts`), `modules/quotes/{quotes.service, quotes.accept.spec, quotes.test-fixtures, quotes.concurrency.integration.spec}.ts`, `modules/requests/{requests.service, requests.service.spec}.ts`, `modules/fulfillment/{fulfillment.service(.spec), project-closure.service(.spec)}.ts`, `modules/admin/admin.controller.ts`, `infra/event-bus/event-bus.service.ts`, `test/{money-services, money-fixtures}.ts`, `.eslintrc.cjs`, `prisma/schema.prisma` (comentariu), 2 migrari; frontend `_components/notification-bell.tsx`, `hooks/use-socket.ts`, `messages/{ro,en}.json`; docs `04-data-model.md`.
- **Decizii (default-uri alese in plan)**:
  - Slot fara RESERVE → tranzitie + penalizare + notificare, fara credite (`SKIPPED_NO_RESERVE`, `refunded=false`) — rezolvat din docs/02 §3.1 + IMPACT credite §2.
  - Firme demo exceptate DOAR de identitatea balance in verificarea zilnica (conventia `cleanup-demo.ts`); numarate in `demoBalanceSkipped`.
  - Alerta: o data pe zi, doar clopotel admin; joburile esuate raman pe `/admin/jobs` (fara alerta noua).

  - [x] `pnpm -F shared build`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint` verzi; jest backend 90 suite / 1056 teste verzi (12 suite de integrare sarite fara DB); Vitest FE 48 / 324 verzi

  - [x] Integrare pe Postgres local `--runInBand`: `credit-settlement` 12/12 (inclusiv (h) rescris, (i) SLA fara RESERVE, (j) portofel deviat), `claims.concurrency` 5/5, `quotes.concurrency` 8/8
  - [x] `pg_indexes`: `credit_transactions_one_reserve_per_slot` + `credit_transactions_one_settlement_per_slot`; `pg_constraint`: ambele `*_nonneg` cu `convalidated = t`
  - [x] Sloturile `e28ed141…`, `1f006ef3…`, `e2438857…`, `ef1051f0…` = SLA_EXPIRED, cheile `claim.sla:<id>` in outbox, notificari `claim.withdrawn` create; `ZCARD bull:sla-breach:failed` = 0; `/admin/jobs` fara sla-breach (jobul claim-assign `c59392fc…` cu aceeasi cauza reincercat → no-op, slot OFFER_SENT)
  - [x] `GET /admin/credits/ledger-check` (admin local) → `walletMismatches: []`, `unsettledTerminalSlots: []`, `demoBalanceSkipped: 8`; owner.a → 403; 0 randuri `credits.ledger_mismatch` in outbox; repeat `ledger-check` inregistrat in Redis (urmatoarea rulare 03:40 Bucuresti)
  - [ ] Linia `ledger-check OK` din consola `nest --watch` — necitita (serverul ruleaza in alta sesiune); jobul de pornire nu a lasat nici esec, nici alerta
- **DECIZIE NECESARA**:

## Sprint 21 (plan 2026-09-28): Money-path tests and transaction policy — 2026-09-29
- **Obiectiv**: fiecare drum al creditelor (claim → retragere / neatribuire / SLA / accept / livrare / stergere) e dovedit pe PostgreSQL real cu ledger-ul echilibrat, testele de integrare nu pot atinge alta baza decat una locala, CI masoara coverage cu praguri pe billing/claims/requests (E-admin-platform-14); nicio tranzactie nu-si mai alege singura izolarea/timeout-ul (`runTx(profil)`), erorile tranzitorii de DB ajung la client ca 503 `DATABASE_BUSY` reincercabil, iar FE reincearca o data actiunile idempotente (E-admin-platform-54). Nicio regula de business, pret sau durata schimbata; docs/07 neatins.
- **Pachete**: E-admin-platform-14, E-admin-platform-54 (detalii in intrarile de pachet de mai jos).
- **Impact**:
  - Backend: harness de test nou `src/test/` (garda de host DB in `globalSetup` + `testDatabaseUrl()`, ceas inghetat, fixturi de bani, servicii reale pe Prisma de test; exclus din build); 3 specuri noi pe Postgres real (settlement credite, curse claim/accept, gating marketplace) + caracterizari unit (trial, `SubscriptionActiveGuard`, aprobare firma, `ClaimsService` cu fabrica tipata). `SizingResult.baseScore`; `POST /requests/estimate` calculeaza `minRon`/`score` din scorul de baza (PO r5 V1; identic azi). Politica unica de tranzactii: `TX_PROFILES` read/write/critical/claim (claim = Serializable 10 s, invarianta 3.1) + `runTx`/`runTxBatch`; toate cele 63 de `$transaction` convertite, ESLint interzice `$transaction` direct in module. `payments.confirm`: membrii cititi inainte de tranzactie, lock per firma inaintea beneficiului, lock-ul seriei doar pe numerotare, `confirmed_at`/`paid_at` stampilate dupa lock (4.17), tot READ COMMITTED. Filtru: P2028/P2024/P1017/P1001 → 503 `DATABASE_BUSY` + `Retry-After: 2`, tracker cu nivel `warning`.
  - Frontend: `useIdempotentApi` reincearca O SINGURA data dupa 2 s cu aceeasi cheie Idempotency-Key la `DATABASE_BUSY` si arata toast-ul `Common.serverBusyRetrying`; i18n +11 chei RO/EN (`DATABASE_BUSY` in 10 namespace-uri `apiErrors`), paritate 3508 = 3508, 0 aparitii "atelier".
  - DB: migrarea `20261001003000_threshold_credit_cost_nullable` (doar `ALTER COLUMN credit_cost DROP NOT NULL`, expand fara DROP; randurile 1/2/4 raman; `seed.ts` nu mai scrie coloana). Aplicata local; pe prod la boot, sigura pe blue/green (imaginea veche citeste doar valori ne-nule existente).
  - Realtime: fara schimbari.
  - Securitate (security close, 8 puncte): fara endpointuri noi, fara upload-uri noi, fara cod de eroare nou; SQL brut nou doar parametrizat (`pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(${...}))`); logurile noi contin doar cod Prisma, ruta-pattern si traceId; mesajul 503 nu expune host-ul DB; garda de test numeste doar host-ul, niciodata credentialele; cheile idempotente FE raman in memorie (nimic in localStorage). **Fix la inchidere**: `payments.concurrency.integration.spec.ts` (E-admin-platform-54, scris in paralel cu garda) citea `process.env.TEST_DATABASE_URL` direct → trece acum prin `testDatabaseUrl()` ca celelalte 10 specuri de integrare (plasa `globalSetup` exista deja; re-rulat pe `marketplace_test`: PASS).
  - Audit: fara mutatii critice noi; auditul existent pe confirmarea platilor neschimbat.

Propunere: `SET LOCAL lock_timeout` = timeout-ul profilului ca prima instructiune in profilele claim/critical (+1 round-trip, ~150 ms cross-region) si SQLSTATE 55P03 → 503 `DATABASE_BUSY`, eventual si timeout pe fetch in FE.

## Sprint 21 / E-admin-platform-54: O singura politica de tranzactii + DB ocupat → 503 reincercabil + FE reincearca o data — 2026-09-30
- **Obiectiv**: nicio tranzactie nu mai alege singura izolarea si timeout-ul; erorile tranzitorii de baza de date (timeout, pool plin, DB inaccesibil, conexiune inchisa) ajung la client ca 503 `DATABASE_BUSY` reincercabil si in tracker ca warning, nu ca bug; confirmarea platilor tine lock-ul seriei de factura doar pe numerotare; FE reincearca automat o data actiunile idempotente. Fara schimbare de schema, **fara migrare**, fara endpoint nou, fara cod de eroare nou (se refoloseste `DATABASE_BUSY` din E-company-2); docs/07 neatins; nicio regula de business, pret sau durata schimbata.
- **Module / tabele**: backend `infra/prisma` (profile + `runTx`), `common/filters`, `infra/monitoring`, billing, claims, quotes, requests, fulfillment, companies, auth, chat, inspiration, uploads, lint; frontend `use-idempotent-api`, i18n. Tabele atinse la rulare (semantica neschimbata): `mock_billing_orders`, `company_credit_wallets`, `credit_transactions`, `subscriptions`, `company_members` (citire), `domain_event_outbox`, `idempotency_keys`.
- **Impact**:
  - Backend, politica: `infra/prisma/tx-options.ts` are `TX_PROFILES` — `read` (RepeatableRead, 5 s/20 s), `write` (READ COMMITTED, 5 s/20 s), `critical` (Serializable, 20 s, retry), `claim` (Serializable, `CLAIM_TX_TIMEOUT_MS` = 10 s, retry; invarianta 3.1). NOU `infra/prisma/tx.ts`: `runTx(prisma, profil, fn)` / `runTxBatch(prisma, ops)`; `isSerializationFailure` / `SERIALIZATION_RETRIES` / `withSerializationRetry` mutate aici (re-exportate din `claims.helpers`; `CLAIM_TX_TIMEOUT_MS` re-exportat din `claims.constants`). P2028/P2024 NU se reincearca in backend.
  - Backend, conversie: toate cele 63 de apeluri `$transaction` din module trec prin `runTx`/`runTxBatch` — 12 `claim` (wrapper-ele `withSerializationRetry` exterioare scoase; `offer-expiry-settlement` si `completeDelivered` castiga retry in proces), 1 `critical` (`runQuoteTransaction`, `QUOTE_TX_OPTIONS` sters), 46 `write`, 4 batch. `REQUEST_WRITE_TX_OPTIONS` scos (comentariul incidentului 2026-09-17 trimite la `TX_PROFILES.write`). ESLint: `$transaction` direct interzis in afara `src/infra/prisma/**`, specuri, fixturi de test si `prisma/**` (mesaj "Foloseste runTx/runTxBatch…"), regulile de logger pastrate (`LOG_RULES`).
  - Plati: `confirm()` citeste membrii firmei inainte de tranzactie; in tranzactie: lock rand → `updateMany` PENDING→CONFIRMED (409 neschimbat) → lock per firma (`billing-company:<id>`) → beneficiu (credite/abonament) → lock serie → MAX()+1 → numar + `confirmed_at`/`paid_at` stampilate DUPA lock (ordinea numerelor = ordinea datelor, 4.17) → outbox. Ramane READ COMMITTED (profil `write`): sub snapshot isolation un confirm care asteapta lock-ul ar citi un MAX vechi. Lock-ul per firma a aparut din testul de integrare: fara el doua confirmari pe aceeasi firma se ciocneau la crearea portofelului (P2002), pentru ca nu mai erau serializate de lock-ul seriei. `cancel`, `expireFromStripeSession`, `adminGrant` = `write` explicit (20 s).
  - Filtru: `P2028`, `P2024`, `P1017` (`PrismaClientKnownRequestError`) si `P1001` (`PrismaClientInitializationError.errorCode`) → 503 `DATABASE_BUSY` + `Retry-After: 2` + warn `<cod> database busy route=… traceId=…`; `captureServerException(err, tags, level)` are nivel optional: DB ocupat pleaca cu `warning`, restul raman `error`.
  - Frontend: `createIdempotentRunner(identity, { onBusyRetry, busyRetryDelayMs })` — la `DATABASE_BUSY` o SINGURA reincercare dupa 2 s cu ACEEASI cheie Idempotency-Key; alte coduri nu se reincearca; la esec final cheia ramane pastrata (5xx). `useIdempotentApi` arata toast-ul `Common.serverBusyRetrying`. Apelurile `api()` simple nu se reincearca automat.
  - i18n: `Common.serverBusyRetrying` ("Serverul e ocupat, reîncercăm…" / "The server is busy, retrying…") + `DATABASE_BUSY` in `Auth`, `Company`, `Lifecycle`, `Fulfillment`, `Billing`, `AdminPayments`, `Admin.requests`, `Admin.calendar`, `Admin.sizing`, `LegalAcceptance` `.apiErrors` (copia existenta, verbatim) — 11 chei noi RO+EN, paritate 3508 = 3508.
- **Fisiere**: backend `.eslintrc.cjs`; `src/infra/prisma/{tx-options.ts, tx.ts (nou), tx.spec.ts (nou)}`; `src/infra/monitoring/error-tracking.ts`; `src/common/filters/all-exceptions.filter(.spec).ts`; `src/modules/billing/{payments.service.ts, payments.concurrency.integration.spec.ts (nou), subscriptions.service.ts, subscription-expiry.processor.ts, billing-readiness.service.ts}`; `src/modules/claims/{claims.constants.ts, claims.helpers.ts, claims.service.ts, claim-assign.processor.ts, clarifications.service.ts, offer-expiry-settlement.processor.ts, reconciliation.processor.ts, sla-breach.processor.ts, withdrawal-reminder.processor.ts, withdrawals.service.ts}`; `src/modules/quotes/{quotes.service.ts, consultation-expiry.processor.ts, quote-validity.processor.ts}`; `src/modules/requests/{requests.service.ts, requests.constants.ts, request-expiration.processor.ts}`; `src/modules/fulfillment/{fulfillment.service.ts, project-cases.service.ts, project-closure.service.ts, project-deadlines.processor.ts}`; `src/modules/{auth/token.service.ts, chat/chat.service.ts, companies/companies.service.ts, inspiration/boards.service.ts, uploads/uploads.service.ts}`; frontend `src/hooks/use-idempotent-api(.test).ts`, `src/messages/{ro,en}.json`.
- **Checklist de acceptare**:
  - [x] `runTx(profil)` fixeaza izolare/timeout/retry; grep-ul `$transaction(` in afara `src/infra/prisma/` (fara specuri) nu gaseste nimic; lint pica pe un `$transaction` direct adaugat temporar intr-un modul (verificat si revenit)
  - [x] Profilul `claim` = Serializable 10 s (3.1), fixat in `tx.spec`
  - [x] P2028/P2024/P1001/P1017 → 503 `DATABASE_BUSY` + `Retry-After: 2` + warn + tracker `warning` (`all-exceptions.filter.spec`)
  - [x] `pnpm -F shared build`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint` verzi; Jest backend complet 86 suite / 1021 teste verzi (10 suite de integrare sarite fara DB); Vitest FE 48 fisiere / 324 teste
  - [x] Integrare pe `marketplace_test` (`--runInBand`, 10 suite / 42 teste): 3 confirmari live concurente cu 150 ms/query → toate reusesc, numere 1-2-3 consecutive, `confirmed_at` in ordinea numerelor, credite acordate o data; plafon 1 s → P2028 → filtrul da 503, comanda ramane PENDING, niciun `credit_transactions`. Rulate in paralel (fara `--runInBand`), suitele de concurenta claims/quotes se calca pe aceeasi baza de test; separat si `--runInBand` trec
  - [x] Live local (owner.a, curl): cererea blocata in psql 15 s → `HTTP/1.1 503`, `Retry-After: 2`, `DATABASE_BUSY`; aceeasi cheie dupa lock → 201, un singur rand `idempotency_keys` (status 201). Slotul creat ramane pe cererea demo locala (6 credite rezervate pentru A Mobila Premium)
  - [x] `useIdempotentApi`: 503 `DATABASE_BUSY` → o reincercare dupa 2 s cu aceeasi cheie; doua 503 → respins dupa 2 fetch-uri, cheia pastrata; 500 → fara reincercare (vitest)
  - [x] Paritate RO/EN
  - [ ] Linia WARN din consola `nest --watch` nu a putut fi citita (serverul de dev ruleaza in alta sesiune); formatul e fixat de spec
  - [ ] Trecerea in browser (toast "Serverul e ocupat, reîncercăm…" in /ro si /en) nu a fost facuta: panoul de browser comun era logat ca alt cont si nu randa; acoperita de vitest
- **DECIZIE NECESARA**: niciuna.

## Sprint 20 (plan 2026-09-28): Pre-claim privacy — 2026-09-29
- **Obiectiv**: inainte de preluare firma vede proiectul, nu omul. Contactele scrise in texte libere sunt ascunse firmelor fara acces (E-admin-platform-45, F2-16), distanta vine dintr-un punct aproximat la ~2 km, pozele pierd EXIF/GPS, fisierele au nume neutre, clientul vede cine are acces la contactele lui, iar politica de confidentialitate spune exact asta (E-client-9: C7-2, C7-3, C7-11, C7-16; SEC1-12 re-verificat).
- **Pachete**: E-admin-platform-45, E-client-9 (detalii in intrarile de pachet de mai jos).
- **Impact**:
  - Backend: screening la publicare/editare (titlu, "Mesaj pentru firme", raspunsuri text, piese) cu politica `pre_claim_contact_text_policy` (MASK implicit / BLOCK → 400 `REQUEST_CONTACT_INFO_IN_TEXT`); titlul manual cu contact e inlocuit de titlul automat; mascare `⟦contact⟧` la citire in marketplace (lista + detaliu) si in `GET /quotes/by-claim` cand `clientVisible = false`. Distanta si acoperirea claim-ului din punctul `snapToPrivacyGrid` (acelasi in SQL si in `assertCoverage`), `distanceKm` intreg sau `null` + `distanceBand` dupa `marketplace_distance_display`. Locatii firma: raza minima 2 km, plafon `company_locations_max` sub advisory lock, limita `company_location_changes_per_day` (24h rolling din audit). Scanul de upload re-encodeaza JPEG/PNG/WEBP fara metadate in chei `clean-<uuid>`, cheile sigilate nu mai contin numele fisierului, firmele vad `attachment-N.ext` (si in Content-Disposition), job `sanitize.legacy` la pornire. Endpointuri noi `GET /requests/:id/visibility` (client proprietar) si `GET /admin/contact-detections` (ADMIN).
  - Frontend: `ContactSafeText` ("contact ascuns" / "contact hidden") pe marketplace, fisa de lucru, `RoomSpecCard`, scene Studio; `ContactInTextHint` non-blocant in configurator; eroarea BLOCK in review; pagina `/admin/contact-detections`; distanta ca km sau banda; formularul de locatii cu raza minima si erorile noi; cardul "Cine vede cererea" pe `/requests/[id]`; avertisment de vizibilitate la pasul Fisiere si in sumar; `Legal.privacy` data/sharing rescrise (telefon, EXIF, ce vad firmele inainte de preluare, emailul contului).
  - DB: migrari `20261001001000_request_contact_detections` (tabel nou + 2 indexuri + FK ON DELETE CASCADE + setare MASK) si `20261001002000_location_privacy_settings` (doar date: 3 randuri `system_settings`). Ambele aditive, `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, aplicate local.
  - Realtime: fara schimbari (notificarile catre firme poarta doar titlul, acum mereu automat).
  - Securitate: endpointurile noi sunt sub guard-urile globale Jwt + Roles, cu DTO validate / `ParseUUIDPipe` si ownership pe cerere (`findOwnedRequest`, respinge si cererile sterse) si pe locatie (`companyId` in where); regexurile detectorului sunt limitate (fara timp patratic pe texte lungi); `sharp` aliniat la `^0.35.4` in package.json + lockfile (`--frozen-lockfile` din CI/Docker trece); `asciiFilename` pastreaza literele fara diacritice in Content-Disposition. Fix la security close: plafon de 100 randuri BLOCKED / cerere / ora in logul de detectari, ca o bucla de reincercari in modul BLOCK sa nu poata umfla tabela.
  - Audit: `@Audit` + `AuditInterceptor` pe rutele de locatii (`COMPANY_LOCATION_CREATED/UPDATED/DELETED`, snapshot doar id-uri + raze, fara adresa/coordonate); setarile noi in allowlist-ul de audit. Logurile noi contin doar id-uri, numar de randuri si politica, fara text sau contacte.

backend `prisma/schema.prisma`, cele 2 migrari, `prisma/seed.ts`, `package.json`, `common/settings/setting-defaults.ts`, `common/privacy/{contact-masking,location-privacy}(.spec).ts`, `infra/storage/storage.service.ts` + `storage.filename.spec.ts`, `modules/requests/{contact-screening(.spec).ts,requests.controller.ts,requests.service.ts,requests.service.spec.ts,requests.visibility.spec.ts,requests.write-rooms.integration.spec.ts}`, `modules/marketplace/marketplace.service(.spec).ts`, `modules/claims/claims.service(.spec).ts`, `modules/companies/{companies.controller.ts,companies.service.ts,dto/company.dto.ts,companies.locations.spec.ts,companies.dashboard.spec.ts}`, `modules/uploads/{image-sanitizer(.spec).ts,uploads.service(.spec).ts,uploads.processor.ts}`, `modules/quotes/{quotes.service.ts,claim-context-access.ts,quotes.claim-context.spec.ts}`, `modules/admin/{admin-contact-detections.controller.ts,admin-contact-detections.service(.spec).ts,dto/admin-contact-detections.dto.ts,admin.module.ts,admin.service.ts,admin-settings-validation.spec.ts}`, `modules/audit/{audit-snapshot(.spec).ts,critical-audit-metadata.spec.ts}`;
