- Admin (4.19): `AdminModule` — `GET /admin/kpi` (firme/cereri pe status, claim-uri active, dispute, plati, abonamente, credite, venit facturat, audit), `GET /admin/audit-logs` (paginat + filtre, 3.9), settings CRUD (`system_settings`, `penalty_rules`, `subscription_plans`, `credit_packages`, `project_size_thresholds` — toate auditate `@Audit`), jobs viewer (`GET /admin/jobs` esuate din toate cozile + `POST /admin/jobs/:queue/:id/retry`, 3.11).
- Seed demo (docs/06 §7): `prisma/seed-demo.ts` (idempotent prin `admin@demo.ro`, parola `Demo1234!`) — 1 admin + 5 clienti numiti + 8 firme cu stari exacte (Gold/Platinum/Silver/expirat, PENDING+risk flags, REJECTED, SUSPENDED 12pct, echipe A&B, B coverage 100km) + 15 cereri cu distributia exacta de statusuri + tipuri + 2 paid-design + 1 oferta EUR + review 5★ si 2★→DISPUTED + 2 facturi + 60 audit + 30 notificari. Verificat: KPI admin reflecta exact datele.
- Frontend: consola admin (`admin/layout.tsx` sidebar + header cu clopotel) + pagini `dashboard` (KPI), `audit` (tabel paginat + filtru), `settings` (editoare system/penalty/plans/packages/thresholds), `jobs` (esuate + retry); clopotel notificari (badge necitite + dropdown + mark-read, poll 30s + invalidare realtime) pe consola admin si dashboard; hooks `use-admin`/`use-notifications`; i18n RO+EN `Admin`/`Notifications`.
- Hardening (security-close): **FIX** audit IP salt folosea fallback hardcodat (`AUDIT_IP_SALT` inexistent) → acum `IP_HASH_SALT` validat din config; **FIX** webhook plata folosea fallback slab `'dev-webhook-secret'` → acum `PAYMENT_WEBHOOK_SECRET` din config (getOrThrow) + rate limit 30/min; **FIX** rate limits per-ruta lipsa (3.13) → claim 10/min, upload 30/min (requests/chat/quote presign), webhook 30/min; **FIX** `@Audit` adaugat pe deciziile admin ramase (company approve/reject, withdrawal review). PASS: guards/RBAC, idempotency, ownership checks, presigned+SAFE, format eroare unic, DTO whitelist.
- Verificat: typecheck shared+backend+frontend OK; JSON i18n valid; migratie aplicata; backend boot OK (toate rutele + DI, ConfigService/AuditInterceptor rezolvate); smoke Phase B 14/14 (admin KPI/settings/audit/penalty/jobs, RBAC 403, notificari processor); seed demo coerent (KPI verificat).
- Presupuneri: seed-demo pe DB proaspat (audit append-only nu permite stergere la re-rulare); blocul penalizare angajat ramane descopat (vezi Sprint 7); admin-in-chat la dispute minimal.

## Sprint 8 — 2026-06-16 — Livrare→COMPLETED + review/dispute + audit + facturare RO
- DECIZIE sesiune: flux complet plata+factura (4.16/3.7, neimplementate pana acum); creditele firmelor care **pierd** oferta se CONSUMA (pay-to-play, fara refund) — rezolva intrebarea deschisa din Sprint 6 (retrofit in `acceptQuote`).
- Prisma: modele noi `reviews`, `review_disputes`, `credit_packages`, `mock_billing_orders`, `audit_logs` + enum-uri `ReviewDisputeStatus`/`BillingOrderType`/`BillingOrderStatus` (nedefinite verbatim, confirmate sesiune); relatii pe `companies`/`requests`. Migratie `delivery_reviews_disputes_billing_audit` cu **trigger DB append-only** pe `audit_logs` (BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE → RAISE EXCEPTION, 3.9). Seed: date furnizor (seller_*) + serie factura + 3 pachete credite.
- Livrare (4.18): `FulfillmentService` — firma castigatoare `POST /company/requests/:id/deliver` (ACCEPTED→DELIVERED_BY_COMPANY); clientul `POST /requests/:id/confirm-delivery` → COMPLETED + slot castigator COMPLETED + **consum credite castigator**.
- Review/dispute (4.18): `POST /requests/:id/review` (client, dupa COMPLETED, o singura data); sub 3★ → `review_disputes` OPEN + request DISPUTED; admin `GET /admin/disputes` + `POST /admin/disputes/:id/resolve` (RESOLVED/DISMISSED → request revine COMPLETED), decizie **auditata**.
- Audit (3.9): `AuditModule` global — `@Audit('action')` + `AuditInterceptor` (asteapta scrierea inainte de raspuns via `concatMap`, audit garantat) + `AuditService` (IP hash-uit cu salt, niciodata date sensibile). Aplicat pe rezolvare dispute + confirmare plata (extensibil in Sprint 9). Viewer-ul UI ramane Sprint 9.
- Facturare/plata (4.16/4.17/3.7): `PaymentsService` — `GET /billing/credit-packages`, `POST /billing/credits/purchase` + `POST /billing/subscription/purchase` (Idempotent) → comanda PENDING cu TVA snapshot (21%); confirmare `POST /admin/payments/:id/confirm` (calea A, auditat) sau `POST /webhooks/payment` (calea B, HMAC-SHA256 + Idempotent + replay) → CONFIRMED + numerotare factura serie-numar secventiala + seller snapshot + grant credite / activare abonament. `InvoicePdfService` (puppeteer, ESM-safe) — factura PDF conforma RO (`GET /billing/orders/:id/invoice`).
- Shared: `reviews.schemas.ts` + `billing.schemas.ts` (Zod + DTO-uri ReviewDto/AdminDisputeItemDto/CreditPackageDto/BillingOrderDto/AdminBillingOrderItemDto + Resolve/Purchase/Webhook inputs); enum-uri noi; coduri noi DELIVERY_NOT_ALLOWED/COMPLETION_NOT_ALLOWED.
- Frontend: hooks `use-billing` (pachete/comenzi/purchase/admin confirm) + `use-fulfillment` (deliver/confirm/review/admin disputes); componenta `client-fulfillment` (confirmare livrare + review pe `/requests/[id]/offers`); buton „marcheaza livrata" pe pagina firmei `/marketplace/claims/[id]`; sectiune cumparare credite + comenzi + descarcare factura pe wallet; pagini admin `/admin/payments` si `/admin/disputes`; i18n RO+EN namespace `Fulfillment`/`Billing`/`AdminPayments`/`AdminDisputes`.
- Verificat: typecheck shared+backend+frontend OK; JSON i18n valid; migratie aplicata (cu trigger) + seed; backend boot OK (toate rutele mapate, DI rezolvat); **smoke E2E pe infra reala — 19/19**: deliver→confirm→COMPLETED+consum credite → review 5★ (fara dispute) → review 2★→DISPUTED→admin resolve→**audit log scris + UPDATE blocat de trigger** → purchase→admin confirm→factura PDF+grant credite → webhook HMAC (valid + semnatura gresita 401) → accept→consum credite pierzator.
- Presupuneri: numerotare factura prin `max(number)+1` sub tranzactie (backstop unique serie+numar); abonament platit = 30 zile; review doar de client dupa COMPLETED; admin-in-chat la dispute (4.19) ramane minimal (resolve + nota) — extindere posibila in Sprint 9.

## Sprint 7 — 2026-06-15 — SLA + clarificari + penalizari (firma) + anulare/retragere claim
- DECIZIE sesiune: **blocarea angajatului (9 pct) e DESCOPATA** — pastram doar penalizarile la nivel de firma (prag 12 → SUSPENDED 6 luni). `penalty_events.scope` ramane in schema, dar MVP creeaza doar scope=COMPANY. (Abatere constienta de la 4.12/D-v6-6.)
- Prisma: modele noi `claim_withdrawals`, `clarification_requests`, `penalty_events`, `penalty_rules`, `business_calendar_holidays` + enum-uri `WithdrawalReasonType`/`WithdrawalStatus` (verbatim §5.5)/`PenaltyScope`/`ClarificationStatus` (PENDING/ANSWERED, confirmat sesiune); `ClaimSlotStatus` += `WITHDRAWN`/`SLA_EXPIRED` (confirmat sesiune); coloane noi `requests.last_edit_at` + `requests.deleted_at` (lipseau din Sprint 4 desi docs/04 le cere). Migratii: `sla_penalties_clarifications_withdrawals`, `request_last_edit_at`, `request_deleted_at`.
- SLA (4.11/D-v6-4): `ClaimsService` materializeaza `sla_deadline_at = addWorkingDays(claim, 3/3/5)` la claim + programeaza job `sla-breach` la deadline+12h grace. `SlaBreachProcessor`: daca slotul e inca ACTIVE (fara oferta) si nepauzat → `SLA_EXPIRED` + consum credite + 3 pct penalizare firma; daca cererea ramane fara claim activ → re-publicare `IN_MARKETPLACE` cu ceas nou (+5 zile lucr., D-v6-13) + excludere firme (`SLA_BREACH`), fara consum repost (Î20).
- Calendar (3.3): `BusinessCalendarService` consulta acum `business_calendar_holidays` (cache in memorie, `onModuleInit`/`refresh`) — sarbatori mobile Paste/Rusalii 2025–2028 (seed) + override admin (punti). API sync pastrat.
- Clarificari (4.11): `ClarificationsService` — firma cere clarificare (`POST /claims/:id/clarifications`) → `slaPausedAt`=now + `sla_deadline_at` +1 zi lucr. + reprogramare job; clientul raspunde (`POST /client/clarifications/:id/answer`) → ANSWERED + reluare SLA; o singura clarificare PENDING odata (`CLARIFICATION_ALREADY_PENDING`).
- Anulare/retragere (4.15): `WithdrawalsService` — motive auto-validate (CLIENT_UNRESPONSIVE_48H pe `chat_threads.last_client_message_at`, REQUEST_MODIFIED_POST_CLAIM pe `last_edit_at`; CLIENT_CONTACT_INVALID/CLIENT_REQUESTED_CANCELLATION mock-auto in MVP) → AUTO_APPROVED + refund + slot `WITHDRAWN`; VOLUNTARY_NO_REASON cu gratie 30 min (refund + 0 pct sub 30 min; altfel fara refund + 2 pct + `WITHDRAWN_VOLUNTARY`); CUSTOM → `PENDING_ADMIN_REVIEW` (slot ramas ocupat) + job reminder 48h (`withdrawal.reminder`, fara auto-decizie); admin decide (`/admin/withdrawals` + `:id/review`). Stergere cerere de client (Î17): `DELETE /requests/:id` → soft delete + claim-uri active `CANCELLED_BY_CLIENT` + refund.
- Penalizari (4.12): `PenaltiesService.applyPenalty` (fereastra rolling 180 zile, expirare individuala) + prag firma 12 → SUSPENDED N luni; `penalty_rules` seed (SLA_MISS=3, MASS_SLA_MISS=3, VOLUNTARY_WITHDRAWAL=2); `GET /penalties/me` (accesibil si firmelor SUSPENDED, fara CompanyApprovedGuard).
- Fix (preexistent Sprint 5, descoperit la smoke): query-ul raw de claim folosea `project_score` (coloana reala `size_score`) si `id = $1::uuid` (id e TEXT) → ambele aruncau 500; corectat (`size_score AS project_score`, fara cast). `POST /claims` rulat E2E pentru prima data.
- Jobs BullMQ noi: `sla-breach`, `withdrawal-reminder`.
- Shared: `claims-lifecycle.schemas.ts` (Zod withdrawal/clarification + DTO-uri ClaimWithdrawalDto/ClarificationRequestDto/PenaltyEventDto/CompanyPenaltyStatusDto/AdminWithdrawalItemDto); `CLAIM_SLOT_STATUSES`+`CLARIFICATION_STATUSES`; `ClaimSlotDto`/`ClaimQuoteContextDto` extinse cu SLA; coduri noi WITHDRAWAL_ALREADY_DECIDED/CLAIM_NOT_WITHDRAWABLE/REQUEST_NOT_DELETABLE.
- Frontend: hooks `use-claims-lifecycle` (clarificari/retrageri/admin) + `use-penalties`; componente `claim-lifecycle-panel` (SLA badge + clarificare + retragere) si `client-clarifications` (raspuns client); SLA/retragere pe pagina firmei `/marketplace/claims/[id]`, raspuns clarificari pe `/requests/[id]/offers`, banner penalizari pe wallet, pagina admin `/admin/withdrawals`, buton stergere pe detaliul cererii; i18n RO+EN namespace `Lifecycle`/`Penalties`/`AdminWithdrawals`.
- Verificat: typecheck shared+backend+frontend OK; JSON i18n valid; 3 migratii aplicate + seed (3 reguli penalizare + 12 sarbatori); backend boot OK (toate rutele mapate, DI rezolvat); **smoke E2E pe infra reala — 19/19**: claim cu SLA materializat → clarificare (pauza+reluare) → retragere voluntara dupa gratie (2 pct) → retragere CUSTOM + admin approve → stergere cerere client → SLA breach (worker real) → republicare + excludere + suspendare la 12 pct → `penalties/me`.
- Presupuneri: SLA = termen de trimitere oferta; clarificarea adauga +1 zi lucr. si pauzeaza (nu insumeaza durata reala a pauzei); ratare in masa = cand requestul ramane fara claim activ dupa o ratare; reasons fara dovada reala auto-aprobate (mock MVP).

## Sprint 6 — 2026-06-15 — Chat realtime + ofertare structurata (versiuni, block, reofertare, PDF)
- Prisma: modele `quotes`, `quote_versions`, `quote_change_requests`, `quote_validity_extensions`, `physical_consultation_invites`, `messages` + enum-uri `quote_currency`/`quote_status` (DRAFT/SENT/WITHDRAWN/EXPIRED/ACCEPTED/SUPERSEDED)/`quote_change_request_status` (PENDING/FULFILLED/REJECTED)/`physical_consultation_invite_status` (verbatim); coloana `chat_threads.last_client_message_at`; `claim_slots.quote_id` ramane denormalizat (index partial 1-claim-fara-oferta). Atasamentele oferta/mesaj trec prin tabelul generic `attachments` (entity_type QUOTE_VERSION/MESSAGE). Migratie `chat_messages_quotes_offers`. NB: `quotes` e 1—N per slot (retragere → WITHDRAWN, firma poate re-oferta).
- Ofertare (4.13): `QuotesService` — v1 (`POST /quotes`, Idempotent), cerere modificare client (`POST /client/quotes/changes`), raspuns firma cu versiune noua (`/quotes/:id/revise`, Idempotent, marcheaza change FULFILLED, max 3 inlantuite → `QUOTE_VERSION_LIMIT_REACHED`), refuz modificare (`/changes/:id/reject` → REJECTED, nu consuma slot; aceeasi modificare nu poate fi reluata → `CHANGE_REQUEST_ALREADY_REJECTED`).
- Block dupa v3 (D3): varianta suplimentara voluntara (`/quotes/:id/extra`, `extra_versions_count++`), invitatie la sediu (`/quotes/:id/consultation-invites`, expira +7 zile cal. via job → EXPIRED), inchidere negociere online (`/quotes/:id/end-negotiation` → chat read-only ambii).
- Reofertare 2 butoane (D4): A `/quotes/:id/extend-validity` (extinde valid_until, max 2 × N zile, audit in `quote_validity_extensions`, nu consuma slot); B `/quotes/:id/reoffer` (versiune noua initiata de firma la expirare, consuma slot, sub limita de 3).
- Valabilitate (D-v6-7): `valid_until = trimitere + quote_validity_default_days` (14 cal., configurabil sau setat de firma); job `quote-validity` la `valid_until` → quote EXPIRED daca nu a fost extinsa/inlocuita.
- Moneda (D-v6-12): `quotes.currency` RON/EUR; DTO expune `priceRon`/`priceEur`/`designFeeRon`/`designFeeEur` cu `eur_ron_rate` (5.2); suma contractuala in moneda firmei, conversia informativa.
- Design fee (D-v6-11): `quote_versions.design_fee` doar daca `requests.includes_paid_design`; altfel respins.
- Permisiuni campuri oferta (Î5/4.13): `assertFieldPermissions` verifica matricea `company_offer_field_permissions` per rol pentru fiecare camp atins (designFee sub PRICE) → `OFFER_FIELD_NOT_EDITABLE`; EMPLOYEE_MANAGED nu poate oferta.
- Retragere oferta (4.13): `/quotes/:id/withdraw` in 1 zi lucratoare (`BusinessCalendarService.addWorkingDays`) → quote WITHDRAWN + slot revine ACTIVE; dupa fereastra → `QUOTE_WITHDRAW_WINDOW_CLOSED`.
- Acceptare (4.14/Î19): `POST /client/quotes/:id/accept` (Idempotent) → request ACCEPTED, quote ACCEPTED, ofertele celorlalte firme → SUPERSEDED + chat-urile lor read-only imediat; chat castigator ramane activ. Tranzitii status cerere: ≥1 oferta → OFFERS_RECEIVED, modificare → NEGOTIATION, accept → ACCEPTED (helper forward-only).
- Chat (4.14): `ChatService` + controllere client (`/chat/*`) si firma (`/company/chat/*`); thread auto-creat la claim (Sprint 5), mesaje text + atasamente presigned (entity MESSAGE, max 5, 3.4), `last_client_message_at` (pentru CLIENT_UNRESPONSIVE_48H, Sprint 7), enforce read-only.
- Realtime (3.5): `EventBusService.publish` extins cu `targetUserIds` — `message.created`/`quote.*` se emit DOAR catre participanti (client + membrii firmei), nu broadcast; eveniment `quote.accepted` catre toti participantii cererii.
- PDF (Sprint-0 §9): `QuotePdfService` (puppeteer, Chromium lazy) — `GET /quotes/:id/pdf` + `/client/quotes/:id/pdf`, A4, dubla afisare RON/EUR + linie design_fee.
- Jobs BullMQ noi: `quote-validity`, `consultation-expiry` (ambele notifica participantii la expirare).
- Shared: `quote.schemas.ts` (Zod create/revise/extra/change/extend/consultation + DTO-uri QuoteDto/QuoteVersionDto/QuoteChangeRequestDto/ConsultationInviteDto/ClaimQuoteContextDto) + `chat.schemas.ts` (sendMessage + MessageDto/ChatThreadDto); enum-uri QUOTE_STATUSES/QUOTE_CHANGE_REQUEST_STATUSES; coduri noi QUOTE_ALREADY_SENT/CHANGE_REQUEST_NOT_PENDING (restul erau deja anticipate).
- Frontend: hooks `use-quotes` (firma + client, Idempotency-Key pe create/revise/extra/reoffer/accept), `use-chat` (threads/messages/send/upload), `use-socket` (`useRealtimeSync` invalideaza cache pe evenimente); componente `chat-panel`, `offer-builder` (RHF+Zod), `offer-card` (versiuni, dubla moneda, actiuni per rol, PDF); pagini `/marketplace/claims/[claimSlotId]` (firma: builder/management oferta + chat) si `/requests/[id]/offers` (client: oferte + accept/modificare/consultanta + chat); link-uri din wallet + detaliu cerere; i18n RO+EN namespace `Quotes`+`Chat` (erori mapate pe cod).
- Verificat: typecheck shared + backend + frontend OK; JSON i18n valid; migratie `chat_messages_quotes_offers` aplicata + seed rulat; backend boot OK (DI rezolvat, toate rutele mapate); smoke E2E pe infra reala (Postgres/Redis/Chromium) — flux complet oferta v1 → modificare client → revizuire v2 → acceptare → 409 re-accept → PDF (%PDF) → chat mesaj firma↔client, toate verde. Fix: puppeteer 25 e ESM-only — import dinamic via `new Function` ca TS (commonjs) sa nu-l transforme in `require()` (ERR_REQUIRE_ESM). puppeteer+Chromium + socket.io-client instalate.
- Presupuneri: o oferta deschisa per slot (status SENT/EXPIRED), iteratiile sunt versiuni; interactiunea client (chat/oferte) cere CLIENT autentificat care detine cererea (request.client_user_id) — cererile anonime pe token nu negociaza in Sprint 6 (consistent cu rutele owner-authenticate amanate din Sprint 4; seed-ul Sprint 9 foloseste conturi numite); creditele firmelor care pierd oferta raman `reserved` (consume/refund definitiv in fluxul de finalizare Sprint 8); `clarification_requests` (SLA-pausing) raman Sprint 7; `@Audit` infra AMANATA Sprint 9.

## Sprint 5 — 2026-06-14 — Marketplace + Claim (eligibilitate, gating, credite, concurrency)
- Prisma: modele `subscription_plans`, `subscriptions`, `company_credit_wallets`, `credit_transactions`, `claim_slots`, `chat_threads`, `request_company_exclusions`, `idempotency_keys` + enum-uri `subscription_plan_tier`/`subscription_status`/`credit_transaction_type`/`claim_slot_status` (6 valori verbatim docs/04)/`request_exclusion_reason`; migratie `marketplace_claims_subscriptions_credits`. Indecși partiali UNIQUE: 1-claim-activ-fara-oferta per assignee (`status='ACTIVE' AND quote_id IS NULL`) + 1-claim per firma per cerere (`status IN ACTIVE/OFFER_SENT/COMPLETED`).
- Claim concurrency (3.1): `ClaimsService.create` în `$transaction` Serializable, timeout 10s, `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` raw pe rândul `requests`; pași: lock → status ∈ {IN_MARKETPLACE, CLAIMED_PARTIAL} (`CLAIM_NOT_ALLOWED`) → sloturi ocupate < max (`CLAIM_SLOTS_FULL`) → excludere (`COMPANY_EXCLUDED_FROM_REQUEST`) → claim propriu existent (`CLAIM_ALREADY_EXISTS`) → acoperire Haversine vs `coverage_radius_km` (`OUT_OF_COVERAGE_AREA`) → reguli atribuire (1-claim/cap manager) → insert slot + `chat_thread` + rezervare credite + recalcul status cerere. `mapUniqueViolation` backstop P2002 pe indecșii partiali.
- Idempotenta (3.2): `@Idempotent()` + `IdempotencyInterceptor` pe `POST /claims` — header `Idempotency-Key` obligatoriu, hash sha256({body,userId}), dedup 24h pe `(key,endpoint)`; același key+hash → răspuns cache; key+hash diferit → `IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT` (409).
- Gating per plan (4.8/4.10): `SubscriptionActiveGuard` atașează `gatingDelayMinutes` din `marketplace_gating_delay_minutes` (Platinum +0 / Gold +30 / Silver +60); marketplace ascunde cererea până la `published_at + delay`.
- Credite (4.5): cost snapshot SMALL=1/MEDIUM=2/LARGE=4 înghețat pe `claim_slot`; `CreditsService` tx-aware (`balance`=liber, `reserved`=blocat) cu GRANT/RESERVE/REFUND/CONSUME + `credit_transactions`; `RESERVE` aruncă `INSUFFICIENT_CREDITS` (402) dacă sold insuficient.
- Trial (4.16): `SubscriptionsService.startTrialIfEnabled` rulat în tranzacția de aprobare admin (retrofit `companies.service`) — creează abonament trial Gold + grant 10 credite bonus, condiționat de `system_settings` (`trial_enabled`/`trial_plan`/`trial_duration_days`/`trial_bonus_credits`).
- Atribuire (4.9): self-assign la creare sau cap manager (sloturi neatribuite ≤ angajați liberi); reatribuire `POST /claims/:id/assign` (OWNER/MANAGER) cu check 1-claim (`ASSIGNED_USER_HAS_ACTIVE_CLAIM`); job BullMQ `claim-assign` la +1h → neatribuit → `CANCELLED_UNASSIGNED` + refund + recalcul status + `claim.withdrawn`.
- Marketplace (4.2): `MarketplaceService` list/detail via `$queryRaw` cu Haversine SQL + `CROSS JOIN LATERAL` pe `company_locations` (filtru `coverage_radius_km`), gating (`make_interval`), excludere `NOT EXISTS`, ocupate < max; **fără date de contact** în DTO-uri pre-claim.
- Guards: `CompanyApprovedGuard` (atașează `companyContext`; `COMPANY_NOT_APPROVED`/`COMPANY_SUSPENDED`) + `SubscriptionActiveGuard` (`SUBSCRIPTION_INACTIVE`) pe rutele `@Roles(COMPANY_USER)` marketplace/claims; `@CurrentCompany` decorator.
- Settings: `SettingsModule` global (`getString/getInt/getNumber/getBool`) peste `system_settings`; seed `subscription_plans` (SILVER 149/15cr/60min, GOLD 399/50cr/30min, PLATINUM 899/120cr/0min) + 13 settings (idempotent, fără overwrite valori ajustate de admin).
- Shared: `marketplace.schemas.ts` (Zod createClaim/assignClaim + DTO-uri MarketplaceItemDto/MarketplaceDetailDto/ClaimSlotDto/CreditWalletDto/SubscriptionDto/ClaimedRequestDto); enums noi SUBSCRIPTION_PLAN_TIERS/SUBSCRIPTION_STATUSES/CREDIT_TRANSACTION_TYPES/REQUEST_EXCLUSION_REASONS.
- Frontend: hooks TanStack `use-marketplace` (marketplace/detail/claim cu `Idempotency-Key`/assign/myClaims/wallet/subscription); pagini `/marketplace` (listă carduri cu distanță/credite/sloturi/gating-ago), `/marketplace/[id]` (detaliu + buton claim cu validare credite/sloturi), `/marketplace/wallet` (portofel + abonament + revendicări); link „Marketplace” pe dashboard firmă (status APPROVED); i18n RO+EN namespace `Marketplace` (erori mapate pe cod).
- Verificat: typecheck shared + backend + frontend OK; migratie aplicată; seed rulat (3 planuri + 13 settings); shared rebuild pentru exporturi noi.
- Presupuneri: `balance` = credite libere și `reserved` = blocate la claim (refund la anulare, consume la finalizare); marketplace listează doar cereri `IN_MARKETPLACE`/`CLAIMED_PARTIAL` cu cel puțin o locație firmă în rază; cap manager = sloturi neatribuite active ale firmei + 1 ≤ angajați liberi (interpretarea 4.9); chat thread creat la claim dar funcțional în Sprint 6.

## Sprint 4 — 2026-06-13 — Cereri client (draft anonim, scoring, geocoding, upload, expirare)
- Prisma: modele `requests`, `request_versions`, `request_rooms`, `request_items`, `request_contact_preferences`, `attachments` (generic entity_type/entity_id), `project_sizing_config`, `project_size_thresholds`, `geocoding_cache` + enum-uri `request_status`/`budget_range`/`room_type`/`material`/`item_system`/`attachment_status`/`project_size` (verbatim docs/04 + enums.ts); migratie `requests_rooms_items_sizing_geocoding`.
- Draft anonim cu token (3.x): `POST /requests/drafts` generează token (sha256 stocat în `draft_token_hash`), `OptionalJwtAuthGuard` leagă draftul de client dacă e logat; GET/PATCH draft pe token; un draft are status `DRAFT`.
- Publicare: validează conținut complet (Zod/DTO), geocodează adresa, calculează scoring + cost credite, setează `IN_MARKETPLACE` + `published_at` + `expires_at` (5 zile lucrătoare), scrie snapshot `request_versions` v1, programează job BullMQ expirare, emite `request.status_changed` prin EventBus.
- Scoring (docs/sprint-0 §7): `SizingService` citește `project_sizing_config` (greutăți ROOM_TYPE/ROOM_SIZE/MATERIAL/SYSTEM/ITEM_QUANTITY/PAID_DESIGN/BUDGET) + `project_size_thresholds` (SMALL 0–14→1cr, MEDIUM 15–29→2cr, LARGE 30+→4cr); seed idempotent `prisma/seed.ts` (tsx).
- Geocoding (3.8): `GeoService` Nominatim (user-agent custom, countrycodes=ro, timeout 8s) cu cache 90 zile în `geocoding_cache`; eșec → `GEOCODING_FAILED`.
- Calendar business (3.3): `BusinessCalendarService` Europe/Bucharest, weekend + sărbători legale RO cu dată fixă, `addWorkingDays` cu conversie corectă EET/EEST via Intl pentru sfârșitul zilei lucrătoare.
- Expirare: coadă `request-expiration` (job întârziat la `expires_at`); `RequestExpirationProcessor` trece `IN_MARKETPLACE` → `EXPIRED` doar dacă nu e preluată, emite eveniment.
- Editare (docs/03): 3 editări pre-claim (`IN_MARKETPLACE`), 1 post-claim (`CLAIMED_*`); blocaj după oferte (`EDIT_BLOCKED_OFFER_RECEIVED`); altfel `REQUEST_NOT_EDITABLE`; re-geocodare doar dacă adresa s-a schimbat; snapshot versiune la fiecare editare. Repost cerere expirată o singură dată (`REPOST_ALREADY_USED`).
- Upload presigned (3.4): `UploadsService` generic — presign PUT (15min) → confirm via `objectExists` (PENDING_UPLOAD → SAFE, fără AV real în MVP) → download presigned (5min); max 10 atașamente/cerere (`FILE_LIMIT_REACHED`), tipuri jpeg/png/webp/pdf, max 10MB; rute scope-uite pe token.
- Guards: rute draft `@Public` + `OptionalJwtAuthGuard`; liste client `@Roles(CLIENT)` (owner check în service). CompanyApprovedGuard rămâne pentru Sprint 5.
- Shared: `request.schemas.ts` (Zod content/patch/edit/contact/room/item + presign/confirm + DTO-uri RequestDto/RequestListItemDto/RequestRoomDto/RequestItemDto/AttachmentDto/RequestSizingDto/RequestDraftCreatedDto/PresignUploadResultDto); enums noi BUDGET_RANGES/ROOM_SIZE_BUCKETS/ITEM_QUANTITY_BUCKETS.
- Frontend: hooks TanStack `use-requests` (draft/publish/edit/repost/upload/list); wizard `/requests/new` (RHF+Zod, `useFieldArray` camere+iteme imbricate, multi-select sisteme, upload presigned direct în storage, token în localStorage); listă `/requests` + detaliu `/requests/[id]` (cu repost pe token); i18n RO+EN namespace `Requests` (validări + erori mapate pe cod).
- Verificat: typecheck shared + backend + frontend OK; migratie aplicată; seed rulat (22 config + 3 praguri); JSON i18n valid.
- Presupuneri: agregarea scorului (Σ camere [ROOM_TYPE+ROOM_SIZE+ITEM_QUANTITY+Σ iteme(MATERIAL+max SYSTEM)] + PAID_DESIGN + BUDGET) e interpretarea deterministică a §7 (greutățile sunt verbatim); calendar business fără sărbători mobile (Paște/Rusalii) și fără override admin — AMÂNAT la Sprint 7; atașamente fără scanare AV reală (status PENDING_SCAN/BLOCKED rezervat); editare/repost autorizate pe token (client logat reține tokenul per-cerere în localStorage) — rute owner-authenticate AMÂNATE.

## Sprint 3 — 2026-06-13 — Firme (onboarding, verificare, locații, echipe, permisiuni)
- Prisma: modele `companies`, `company_members`, `company_locations`, `company_verification_profiles`, `company_portfolio_items`, `company_offer_field_permissions` + enum-uri `company_status`/`company_member_role`/`offer_field_key` (verbatim docs/04 §5.5); migratie `companies`.
- Onboarding firmă: COMPANY_USER fără firmă creează firma și devine OWNER; tranzacție creează firma + membru OWNER + verification profile (risk flags snapshot) + seed matrice permisiuni câmpuri ofertă. Un user = o singură firmă (`@@unique userId`).
- Verificare + reaplicare: CUI normalizat (drop prefix RO); CUI deja folosit de firmă ne-REJECTED → `CUI_ALREADY_REGISTERED`; blocaj reaplicare 3 luni pe CUI **sau** email owner după REJECTED → `COMPANY_REAPPLY_BLOCKED` (4.6).
- Risk flags automate (4.6): `NO_PORTFOLIO` (0 itemi) + `INSUFFICIENT_REVIEWS` (reviews=0 < prag 3); `LOW_RATING` rezervat (reviews vin în Sprint 8). Adminul vede flag-urile dar aprobă manual oricum.
- Locații cu `coverage_radius_km` (default 50, D-v6-10): CRUD per firmă, doar OWNER/MANAGER.
- Echipă (4.7): adăugare membru existent după email (rol MANAGER/EMPLOYEE_TRUSTED/EMPLOYEE_MANAGED), schimbare rol, eliminare; ultimul OWNER nu poate fi retrogradat/eliminat (`LAST_OWNER_CANNOT_LEAVE`).
- Portofoliu: titlu/descriere/imageUrl (fără upload real — flow presigned vine în Sprint 4).
- Matrice permisiuni câmpuri ofertă (Î5/4.13): seed default la onboarding (owner/manager = orice; trusted = orice mai puțin PRICE; managed = read-only); doar OWNER ajustează (upsert pe `companyId+role+fieldKey`).
- Admin (4.6/4.19): `GET /admin/companies?status=`, `GET /admin/companies/:id` (cu risk flags + numărători), `POST /:id/approve`, `POST /:id/reject` (motiv obligatoriu); decizia + reviewer stocate pe verification profile.
- Guards: `RolesGuard` — rutele firmă `@Roles(COMPANY_USER)`, rutele admin `@Roles(ADMIN)`; autorizarea fină pe rol în firmă (OWNER/MANAGER) în service.
- Shared: `company.schemas.ts` (Zod onboarding/locație/membru/portofoliu/permisiuni + DTO-uri CompanyDto/AdminCompanyListItemDto/CompanyRiskFlag).
- Frontend: hooks TanStack (`use-company`) cu `setQueryData` din CompanyDto returnat de fiecare mutație; pagină `/company` (onboarding RHF+Zod sau dashboard cu profil/locații/echipă/portofoliu/matrice), zonă admin `/admin/companies` (listă cu filtru status + detaliu approve/reject); i18n RO+EN (`Company`, `AdminCompanies`) + erori mapate pe cod.
- Verificat: typecheck shared + backend + frontend OK.
- Presupuneri: trial Gold + 10 credite la approve (4.16) AMÂNAT la sprintul de abonamente; audit @Audit (3.9) AMÂNAT la Sprint 9 (decizia + reviewer persistate pe verification profile); risk flags fără rating/reviews până în Sprint 8; portofoliu fără upload real până în Sprint 4.

## Sprint 2 — 2026-06-13 — Auth
- Prisma: modele `users` + `refresh_tokens` (enum-uri UserRole/Language verbatim docs/04), migratie `auth_users_refresh_tokens`.
- Auth backend: register (bcrypt cost 12, rol CLIENT/COMPANY_USER), email verify mock (token in Redis 24h, link via Mailpit), login cu timing uniform + EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED, refresh token rotation cu familie + grace 30s (reuse dupa grace → revoca familia, REFRESH_TOKEN_REUSED), sesiune unica (login nou revoca tot + `auth_expired` pe socketul vechi), logout (revoca familia), GET /auth/me.
- 2FA TOTP (otplib v12): rute setup/verify implementate, blocate de flag `TWO_FACTOR_FEATURE_ENABLED=false`; TwoFactorGuard no-op (invarianta 3.13).
- Tokens DOAR in cookies httpOnly (`mm_access` 15min, `mm_refresh` 7d pe /api/v1/auth); cookie-parser global.
- Guards globale via APP_GUARD: ThrottlerGuard (100/min global, 5/min login) → JwtAuthGuard (@Public pe rutele deschise + health) → RolesGuard (@Roles).
- Socket.IO: auth pe handshake din cookie (3.5) — token lipsa/expirat → `auth_expired` + disconnect; room `user:{id}`; EventBusService.emitAuthExpired.
- Shared: `auth.schemas.ts` (loginSchema/registerSchema Zod + AuthUser).
- Frontend: client API cu cookies + refresh-once dedupe pe 401, hooks TanStack (useMe/useLogin/useRegister/useLogout/useVerifyEmail), pagini login/register/verify-email + dashboard protejat client-side, erori mapate pe cod (3.10), i18n RO (diacritice) + EN.
- Verificat E2E: register→Mailpit→verify→login→me→refresh→logout; reuse dupa grace revoca familia (fix: revocarea scoasa din $transaction ca sa nu fie anulata de rollback la throw); sesiune unica; 2FA 403 flag off; rate limit 429 la a 6-a incercare/min.
- Presupuneri: access 15min/refresh 7d; login cere email verificat; token verify in Redis (fara coloane noi).

## Sprint 1 — 2026-06-13 — Setup monorepo + infra
- Monorepo pnpm workspaces: `apps/backend`, `apps/frontend`, `packages/shared` (+ tsconfig.base, scripts root).
- `packages/shared`: ERROR_CODES const + tipuri, enum-uri canonice din docs/04 §5.5 (verbatim), `ApiErrorBody`.
- `dev-infra/docker-compose.yml`: Postgres 16, Redis 7, MinIO (+ init bucket `uploads`), Mailpit — toate cu healthcheck.
- Backend NestJS: env Zod fail-fast (`config.schema.ts`), prefix global `api/v1`, Helmet + CORS strict, ValidationPipe global, filter erori format 3.10 cu traceId, logging pino JSON, Socket.IO cu Redis adapter (invarianta 3.5), BullMQ root + coada `notifications`, EventBusService.publish skeleton, StorageService (presigned 15min/5min), MailService (Mailpit), PrismaService.
- Prisma: prima migratie `init_system_settings` (model SystemSetting — docs/04 §5.4).
- Frontend Next.js 14 App Router: next-intl ro+en (middleware + `[locale]`), Tailwind, TanStack QueryClient provider, landing minimal bilingv.
- Verificat: typecheck ambele appuri, `next build`, infra up, `GET /api/v1/health` → db/redis/storage `up`.
